### [204] [RATIO: A Benchmark for Retrieval Across Typed Ideation Operations in Scientific Literature](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27394)
*Maayan Sharon,Tom Hope*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出RATIO（Retrieval Across Typed Ideation Operations），一个大规模科学文献检索基准，通过三种"构思操作"（Address/Broaden/Specify）定义相关性，旨在支持人类和AI科学家从科学文献中获取不同抽象层级的灵感。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 检索到的科学文献可以作为人类和AI科学家的灵感来源，但灵感可以有不同形式：既有直接解决当前问题的方法，也有从更抽象/更具体层面提供的启示。现有检索基准仅基于直接相关性定义，缺乏对不同"构思层级"（generalization vs. specialization）的建模。

Method: 构建RATIO基准：(1) 利用话语标记远程监督（discourse-marker distant supervision）从数百万篇CS全文本论文中自动生成候选数据；(2) 将原本仅用于分类的远程监督方法扩展到语料库规模检索；(3) 结合大规模LLM筛选和人工审核提升数据质量；(4) 定义三种构思操作——Address（针对已陈述问题检索方法）、Broaden（检索更一般化表述）、Specify（检索具体实例化）。

Result: 实验表明操作特定的微调能显著提升检索器性能，但仍有较大的改进空间。RATIO作为可扩展的训练与评估框架，为文献驱动的构思（ideation）检索组件提供了基准支持。

Conclusion: RATIO为科学灵感检索开辟了新的研究方向，提供了一个覆盖不同抽象层级（问题解决、泛化、具体化）的可扩展检索基准，有助于未来构建更有效的科学构思支持系统。

Abstract: Retrieved scientific literature can serve as inspiration for both human and AI scientists. Inspiration can take different forms: prior work may directly suggest how to address a problem, or surface directions at different levels of abstraction - zooming out to a more general view or zooming in to a concrete realization. We introduce RATIO (Retrieval Across Typed Ideation Operations), a large-scale benchmark in which relevance is defined by three operations which we name ideation moves: Address retrieves potential approaches for stated problems, Broaden retrieves more general formulations, and Specify retrieves concrete instantiations. RATIO is constructed from millions of full-text scientific papers across CS literature via a general recipe that extends discourse-marker distant supervision - previously used only for classification - to corpus-scale retrieval, combined with extensive LLM and human vetting. Experiments show that operation-specific fine-tuning substantially boosts retrievers but leaves much room for further improvements. RATIO provides a scalable training and evaluation framework for retrieval components that support literature-grounded ideation, opening up new research avenues on scientific inspiration retrieval.

</details>


### [205] [Making Clinical Language Models Auditable: Concept-Guided Fine-Tuning for Robust Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27397)
*Jin Mu,Guanhua Chen*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: CAST是一个基于稀疏自编码器（SAE）的框架，用于可审计的临床文本分类。它通过暴露和抑制笔记特异性伪影（如模板、分隔符、套话）来提升模型在部署分布偏移下的鲁棒性，并在MIMIC-IV数据集上优于微调编码器基线，同时提供特征级审计追踪。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 临床语言模型在院内数据上表现优异，但部署时因依赖笔记特异性伪影（而非真实的患者状态）导致分布偏移下性能下降。现有方法缺乏对伪影特征的识别与抑制机制，也无法提供可审计的决策依据。

Method: CAST框架包含四个核心步骤：（1）使用稀疏自编码器（SAE）从Transformer中间激活中提取稀疏、可人类审计的特征；（2）通过LLM辅助的解读管道结合ICD-10检索约束对SAE潜在变量进行标注；（3）在微调过程中通过残差减法抑制已验证的伪影潜在变量；（4）提供事后逐概念归因以审计模型决策。

Result: 在MIMIC-IV出院记录死亡预测任务上，CAST优于对应的微调编码器基线，并与强LLM基线保持竞争力，同时生成特征级审计轨迹，展示支持每次预测的临床概念以及在训练中抑制的伪影概念。

Conclusion: CAST通过SAE实现概念引导的伪影抑制，兼顾了模型在分布偏移下的鲁棒性和决策的可审计性，为临床文本分类提供了一种兼顾性能与可信度的解决方案。

Abstract: Clinical language models can achieve strong in-hospital accuracy yet fail under deployment shifts because they exploit note-specific artifacts (e.g., templates, separators, boilerplate) that do not reflect patient state. We propose CAST (Concept-guided Artifact Suppression Tuning), an SAE-based framework for auditable clinical text classification. CAST uses Sparse Autoencoders to expose sparse, human-auditable features from intermediate Transformer activations, labels SAE latents with an LLM-assisted interpretation pipeline and ICD-10 retrieval constraints, suppresses verified artifact latents via residual subtraction during fine-tuning, and provides post-hoc per-concept attributions for auditing model decisions. On MIMIC-IV discharge-note mortality prediction, CAST improves over its corresponding fine-tuned encoder baselines and remains competitive with strong LLM baselines, while producing a feature-level audit trail of the clinical concepts that support each prediction and the artifact concepts suppressed during training.

</details>


### [206] [How Language Models Organize and Structure Moral Knowledge](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27402)
*Orion Reblitz-Richardson*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文研究大语言模型如何组织和表示道德知识：通过为道德基础理论（MFT）的六个类别各训练一个线性探针，发现这些探针方向在表示空间中既不坍缩为一个单一道德检测器，也不彼此孤立，而是跨越近最大数量的独立维度并共享一个正的公共成分——这是"整合"的标志（平均成对余弦0.26 vs 非道德概念的0.013）。该几何结构在不同架构和规模下一致，且在预训练早期即达到整合状态（早于探针精度饱和）。模型发现的道德结构不支持MFT预测的个体化/约束区分，而是反映语料统计。在道德困境中，每个困境方向可部分由其组成基础构成（比错配基线高2.7倍），其余大部分方差编码冲突特有结构，表明模型表征的是道德张力本身而非预判结论。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究已表明LLM能检测道德内容，但检测只是低门槛。作者想探究一个更深层的问题：LLM是否能更进一步——区分不同的道德基础，并在表示空间中以几何方式组织它们之间的关系？这关系到模型内部是否形成了结构化的道德知识表征。

Method: 在开放权重语言模型上针对MFT的六个类别（care/harm, fair/cheat, lib/oppress, loy/betray, auth/subv, sanc/degrade）各训练一个独立线性探针，然后分析这些探针方向在表示空间中的几何关系（维度独立性、成对余弦相似度等）。同时构建匹配的非道德概念探针作为对照，并在多个架构/规模下验证一致性，进一步分析预训练阶段几何结构的演化，以及将探针扩展到道德困境场景下检验方向组合性。

Result: 六个道德方向的几何结构既不坍缩也不孤立：跨越近最大数量的独立维度，同时共享正公共成分（平均成对余弦0.26 vs 非道德基线0.013）。结构跨架构和规模一致，预训练早期即达整合状态。模型未表现出MFT预测的个体化/约束二分（该检验功效较低，仅20种可能划分），而是反映语料统计。在道德困境中，困境方向从基础方向部分组合而成（2.7倍于错配基线），但多数方差编码冲突特有结构。

Conclusion: LLM确实在内部形成了超越简单道德检测的结构化道德表征：多个道德基础共享一个整合的公共成分但同时保持相互区分。这种几何组织的"整合中区分"模式不是架构巧合，而是模型从语料统计中习得的普遍特性。模型表征道德困境的方式表明它编码的是道德张力本身，而非预判的道德结论。

Abstract: How do large language models (LLMs) organize moral knowledge? Models detect moral content broadly, but detection is a low bar. We ask whether they go further, distinguishing moral foundations from one another and organizing the relationships between them geometrically.
  We train six independent linear probes on open-weight language models, one per Moral Foundations Theory (MFT) category (care/harm, fair/cheat, lib/oppress, loy/betray, auth/subv, sanc/degrade), and examine how the resulting directions relate to each other in representation space. We find the directions neither collapse into a single moral detector nor isolate from one another. Rather, they span a near-maximal number of independent dimensions while sharing a positive common component. The shared component is the signature of integration, and it is moral-specific relative to a matched non-moral concept battery built identically (mean pairwise cosine 0.26 vs. 0.013).
  The geometry is consistent across architectures and scale and reaches its integration regime early in pre-training, well before probe accuracy saturates. The structure the model discovers shows no evidence of the individualizing/binding distinction predicted by Moral Foundations Theory (an underpowered test: only 20 candidate partitions exist) but rather reflects corpus statistics. Extending to moral dilemmas, each dilemma direction partially composes from its component foundations, at 2.7x a mismatched-pair baseline, while the majority of its variance encodes conflict-specific structure. The model represents moral tension itself, not a pre-resolved judgment.

</details>


### [207] [Consolidating RLVR Capabilities Across Domains: A Deep Dive into Fusion Paradigms](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27409)
*Siye Wu,Kai Yang,Yuchen Cai,Xin Xu,Peng-Yuan Wang,Jiaxuan Wang,Jiashun Liu,Jiafei Lyu,Yangkun Chen,Saiyong Yang,Yanghua Xiao*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文系统比较了三种多领域强化学习融合范式（Merge、Mix RL、MOPD），发现它们平均性能差异不超过1.4分但在单一基准上可相差8.6分，并给出了选择指南。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: RLVR能提升LLM的特定能力，但覆盖多能力通常需要训练多个领域专家并整合。三种融合范式（Merge、Mix RL、MOPD）此前多被孤立研究，缺乏系统性对比和选择依据。

Method: 作者统一了三种融合范式：Merge（合并专家任务向量）、Mix RL（池化数据集训练单一模型）、MOPD（多教师on-policy蒸馏，同时利用专家和数据）。在共享专家与数据的条件下，跨模型规模和多样化的多领域基准套件上进行公平对比。

Result: 三种范式平均性能仅相差至多1.4分，但单一基准上差距可达8.6分；领域层面的性能差异与任务向量几何中可见的跨领域关系相关。训练动态暴露各自约束：Mix RL依赖领域混合比例，MOPD受限于教师上限，Merge将所有专家更新压缩为一个。三者均能提升单样本准确率，但不带来可测的解决方案覆盖率提升，也不损失保留能力。

Conclusion: 实践指南：已有专家且追求低成本融合时选Merge；无专家且需训练统一模型时选Mix RL（需调整领域比例以利用跨领域迁移）；需保留领域特定增益且不追求超越教师或最小化端到端成本时选MOPD。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning with verifiable rewards (RLVR) improves specific capabilities of large language models, but covering multiple capabilities often involves training separate domain experts and subsequently consolidating them. We organize three fusion paradigms by the artefacts they reuse: Merge combines expert task vectors, Mix RL pools their datasets, and multi-teacher on-policy distillation (MOPD) uses both. Because they have largely been studied in isolation, how they compare and how to choose among them remain unclear. We compare all three using shared experts and data across model scales and a multi-domain benchmark suite. Although their average performance differs by at most 1.4 points, the gap reaches 8.6 points on a single benchmark, with domain-level variation tracking cross-domain relations visible in task-vector geometry. Training dynamics expose distinct constraints: Mix RL depends on domain mixture proportions, MOPD remains bounded by its teachers, and Merge compresses all expert updates into one. All three improve single-sample accuracy without measurable gains in solution coverage or losses in held-out capabilities. These results yield a practical guideline: use Merge when experts already exist and cheap fusion is paramount; Mix RL when training a unified model without experts, with domain proportions adjusted for cross-domain transfer; and MOPD when preserving domain-specific gains matters more than surpassing teachers or minimizing end-to-end cost.

</details>


### [208] [Boosting LLM Exploration via Weak-Model Guidance in RLVR](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27420)
*Xingyu Shen,Huishuai Zhang,Peng Li,Yinchun Wang,Dongyan Zhao*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出一种名为"外部前缀"的简单方法：在RLVR训练中，用更小更弱模型生成的部分推理轨迹作为前缀，强制目标模型基于这些不熟悉的片段生成答案，从而保持生成多样性、防止熵坍缩，并显著扩大推理覆盖范围（pass@k在k增大时收益更明显）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: RLVR（可验证奖励强化学习）虽显著提升LLM推理能力，但常导致策略熵下降，造成推理覆盖收窄和较大的k下pass@k退化。现有方法通过算法正则化缓解熵坍缩，但忽略了跨模型的非参数扰动这一途径。

Method: 提出基于外部偏置的简单方法：不依赖模型自身内部探索，而是强制目标模型基于由更小、更弱的语言模型生成的部分推理轨迹（前缀）来生成答案。这些不熟悉的前缀能打破模型过度自信，鼓励探索不同的推理路径。文章还实证研究了外部前缀的作用，揭示分布差异对RLVR训练中探索动态的影响机制。

Result: 在多个数学基准上，该方法一致优于Vanilla RLVR。性能增益随k增大而愈发显著，表明推理覆盖范围得到实质性扩展。此外，该方法有效缓解熵坍缩，且无需额外SFT、复杂奖励设计或复杂提示工程。

Conclusion: 跨模型非参数扰动（外部前缀）是一种简单高效且零成本的方式，能够在不引入复杂机制的前提下保持LLM在RLVR训练中的生成多样性，并有效提升大规模k下的推理覆盖性能。

Abstract: Reinforcement Learning with Verifiable Rewards (RLVR) significantly improves LLM reasoning but often causes a drop in policy entropy, leading to narrowed reasoning coverage and degraded pass@$k$ for large $k$. While existing methods mitigate this entropy collapse through algorithmic regularizations, cross-model non-parametric perturbation is also neglected. In this work, we propose a simple yet effective approach to preserve the generative diversity of LLMs during RLVR. Instead of relying solely on internal exploration, we force the target model to generate answers based on partial reasoning trajectories generated by a smaller, weaker language models. These unfamiliar prefixes effectively disrupt over-confidence and encourage the exploration of distinct reasoning paths. We empirically study the potential of outer prefixes, revealing the mechanism of the impact of distributional discrepancy to the exploration dynamics in RLVR training. Experiments across multiple mathematical benchmarks show that our method consistently outperforms vanilla RLVR. Notably, the performance gain becomes increasingly pronounced as $k$ scales up, demonstrating a substantial expansion of reasoning coverage. Furthermore, our approach efficiently mitigates entropy collapse without requiring additional SFT, intricate reward designs, or complex prompting.

</details>


### [209] [Stochastic Estimation of Transduced Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27428)
*Vésteinn Snæbjarnarson,Samuel Kiegeland,Manuel de Prada Corral,Ryan Cotterell,Tim Vieira*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种用于转导语言模型（TLM）目标前缀概率估计的无偏束求和算法。与先前的阈值剪枝束求和不同，该方法通过无放回重采样源前缀，并以包含概率的倒数进行加权，递归应用此修正得到无偏估计器，同时能够估计阈值剪枝所丢失的概率质量。算法在运行时动态减少保留的源前缀数量，保证以概率1终止。在百科全书文本和DNA数据上的实验表明，该方法在计算-方差权衡、误差以及运行时间上均优于现有基线，并显著加速了DNA到氨基酸转导任务。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 计算TLM中目标前缀的概率需要对所有映射到该前缀的源字符串求和，该集合可能指数级大甚至无限。先前工作使用的阈值剪枝束求和只能给出误差未知的下界。因此需要一种既能高效计算又能提供无偏估计的方法。

Method: 该方法对源前缀进行无放回重采样，并对每个被选中的前缀按其包含概率的倒数进行加权。递归应用此修正即得到目标前缀概率的无偏估计器。束求和算法通过扩展保留的源前缀并采样决定保留哪些前缀，随着更多概率质量被加入估计值，保留的前缀数量动态减少，从而节省计算并保证以概率1终止。

Result: 在百科全书文本任务中，该方法在计算-方差权衡上优于顺序蒙特卡洛基线；在DNA任务中，在相同最大粒子数下误差更低。在DNA到氨基酸转导任务中，相比阈值剪枝束求和，运行时间减少数个数量级，使长目标字符串的前缀概率估计成为可能。在已发表的阅读时间分析中，用无偏采样替换阈值剪枝后，估计的语料惊讶度显著降低，但已发表的结论不变。

Conclusion: 无放回重采样的束求和算法为TLM目标前缀概率提供了一种无偏且高效的估计方法，在计算效率、误差控制和可扩展性方面均优于先前的阈值剪枝方法，同时保持了对下游分析结论的稳健性。

Abstract: Transduced language models (TLMs) compose a pretrained \emph{source} language model with a functional finite-state transducer to induce a language model over \emph{target} strings. Computing the probability of a target prefix under a TLM amounts to summing the source-model probabilities of all source strings that the transducer maps to target strings beginning with that prefix. This set can be exponentially large or infinite. Prior work uses a computational shortcut based on source prefix probabilities, then approximates the resulting sum with threshold-pruned beam summing. This produces a lower bound with unknown error. Instead, we resample source prefixes without replacement and reweight each selected prefix by the inverse of its inclusion probability. We show that applying this correction recursively gives an unbiased estimator of the target prefix probability and lets us estimate the mass lost by threshold pruning. Our beam-summing algorithm extends the retained source prefixes and samples which prefixes to keep, reducing their number as more probability mass is added to the running estimate. This can save computation and guarantees that the run halts with probability one. We evaluate the method on encyclopedic text and DNA against sequential Monte Carlo baselines that resample with replacement. It achieves a better compute--variance tradeoff on text and lower error at the same maximum number of particles on DNA. On a DNA-to-amino-acid transduction, it reduces runtime by several orders of magnitude relative to threshold-pruned beam summing and makes estimating prefix probabilities for long target strings feasible. Replacing threshold pruning with unbiased sampling in a published reading-time analysis substantially lowers the estimated corpus surprisal but leaves the published conclusions unchanged.

</details>


### [210] [TTPO: Test-Time Policy Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27448)
*Aozhe Wang,Zhengxi Lu,Jianze Wang,Shangke Lv,Ying Liu,Weiming Lu,Jun Xiao,Yueting Zhuang,Hua Yang,Qianglong Chen,Yongliang Shen*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出Test-Time Policy Optimization (TTPO)，一种无需真实标签的测试时优化方法，通过非对称目标处理多数票伪标签：对一致的rollout使用OPSD蒸馏，对不一致的rollout使用Grouped RL惩罚，辅以token级选择。在五个竞赛级基准上匹配有标签监督的OPSD，显著提升测试时训练效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的RL和OPSD等后训练方法在数学推理上表现出色，但依赖ground-truth标签，无法用于测试时训练（TTT）。用多数票伪标签替代真实标签虽自然，但存在脆弱性：错误投票会破坏教师模型并误导每个token。

Method: 观察到失败模式的不对称性：与伪标签不一致的rollout通常无论如何都是错误的。基于此，提出TTPO：(1) 非对称目标——对一致的rollout通过OPSD进行蒸馏，对不一致的rollout通过Grouped RL进行惩罚；(2) token级选择——蒸馏时降低已收敛位置的权重，RL只惩罚高置信度的错误；(3) 多数票路由机制，随模型改进提供更紧的自监督信号。

Result: 无需任何标签即可匹配有标签监督的OPSD在五个竞赛级基准上的表现；将Qwen3-1.7B从38.0%提升到45.2%（TTT场景）；无思考模式下获得+25.2%到+36.4%的提升；展现出强跨任务泛化能力。

Conclusion: TTPO通过利用伪标签失败模式的不对称性，结合逐token细粒度更新和多数票路由，实现了在无标签条件下的有效测试时策略优化，弥合了无监督TTT与有监督后训练之间的性能差距。

Abstract: Recent prominent post-training methods, such as Reinforcement Learning (RL) and On-Policy Self-Distillation (OPSD), have driven rapid progress in mathematical reasoning for large language models, yet their reliance on ground-truth labels precludes test-time training (TTT). Replacing ground truth with majority-vote pseudo-labels is a natural alternative, yet it is fragile: an incorrect vote corrupts the teacher and misleads every token. We observe that this failure mode is asymmetric: rollouts that disagree with the pseudo-label are typically wrong regardless of whether the vote itself is correct. Building on this observation, we propose Test-Time Policy Optimization (TTPO), an asymmetric objective that distills agreeing rollouts via OPSD and penalizes disagreeing rollouts with Grouped RL. Token-level selection further refines both branches: distillation down-weights already-converged positions, while RL penalizes only confident errors. Both updates remain well-grounded even under frequent pseudo-label errors, and majority-vote routing yields tighter self-supervision as the model improves. Without any labels, TTPO matches label-supervised OPSD on five competition-level benchmarks, raises Qwen3-1.7B from 38.0% to 45.2% in TTT, yields +25.2% to +36.4% without thinking, and shows strong cross-task generalization.

</details>


### [211] [CritICL: Inference-Time Weak-to-Strong Generalization from Small Language Model Failure Modes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27455)
*Yufan Wu,Yinghui He,Zhengyi Hu,Lang Wei,Ruichen Li,Qifan Yang,Ting Zhu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出CritICL，一种新颖的推理时框架，通过利用较弱模型的失败模式作为引导来提升LLM推理性能，同时保持高效率。它包含动态和静态两种变体，在性能上与测试时扩展方法竞争或更优，但所需生成次数和token成本显著更低。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的推理时扩展方法虽然显著提升了LLM推理能力，但通常依赖重复生成或外部验证，导致计算开销大、效率低。作者的核心洞察是同一模型家族内不同规模的LLM在失败模式上表现出结构化规律，因此可以主动利用这些失败模式而非简单忽略，将其转化为推理引导信号。

Method: CritICL通过将弱模型产生的失败模式融入基于批判的上下文示例来改进推理。提出两种变体：CritICL-dynamic能够自适应预测特定输入的失败模式并检索相应批判；CritICL-static使用全局失败模式画像提供稳定引导。整体框架不需要额外的生成或外部验证，而是将失败转化为可学习的指导信号。

Result: 实验结果表明，CritICL一致性地优于标准上下文学习（ICL），性能与测试时扩展方法（test-time scaling）相当或更优，同时显著减少了生成次数和token消耗。代码已开源。

Conclusion: CritICL证明了失败模式可以被结构化地利用而非简单丢弃，为高效推理增强提供了一条新路径：通过跨模型尺度的失败模式迁移，在避免高昂计算开销的同时获得接近甚至超越测试时扩展方法的推理性能。

Abstract: Recent advances in inference-time scaling have significantly improved the reasoning performance of large language models (LLMs). However, these methods typically rely on repeated generation or external verification. To address this limitation, we introduce CritICL, a novel inference-time framework that improves reasoning while maintaining high efficiency. Our key insight is that LLM failure modes exhibit structured patterns across model scales within the same family. Instead of treating failures as undesirable outputs, CritICL leverages them as a source of guidance. Specifically, we utilize failure modes derived from weaker models and incorporate them into inference through critique-based in-context examples. We propose two variants: CritICL-dynamic, which adaptively predicts input-specific failure modes and retrieves critiques, and CritICL-static, which uses a global failure mode profile to provide stable guidance. Experimental results show that CritICL consistently outperforms standard in-context learning and achieves performance competitive with or superior to test-time scaling methods, while requiring significantly fewer generations and lower token cost. Code available at: https://github.com/umwyf/CRITICL

</details>


<div id='cs.IT'></div>

# cs.IT [[Back]](#toc)

### [212] [Weight Distributions of Single Parity-Check Product Codes via Character Sums](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26457)
*Makson Miller Alves Ribeiro,Sara D. Cardell*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 本文研究二进制单奇偶校验乘积码的结构与枚举性质。确定了SPC(m)⊗SPC(n)的广义Hamming重量谱；对平方乘积码SPC(n)⊗SPC(n)确定了最大码字重量、齐次重量枚举器的对称性条件；通过MacWilliams恒等式的Walsh-Hadamard形式推导出重量枚举器的精确闭式表达式，并利用Krawtchouk多项式提供了无需穷举即可计算完整重量分布的精确程序。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 乘积码在编码理论中具有重要地位，但单奇偶校验乘积码的结构性质，特别是其重量分布和广义重量谱，此前缺乏系统的理论刻画。重量分布是评估码性能的关键指标，而穷举计算在码长增大时不可行，因此需要理论级闭式表达式与高效计算程序。

Method: 作者首先研究乘积码C_{m,n}=SPC(m)⊗SPC(n)的广义Hamming重量层次结构；随后对平方情形C_n确定最大码字重量并证明齐次重量枚举器对称性的充要条件。通过对偶码的刻画，利用MacWilliams恒等式（Walsh-Hadamard形式）推导重量枚举器的精确闭式表达式；将辅助二进制向量按Hamming重量分组，以二项式系数和交替卷积给出每个系数的显式公式；最后利用Krawtchouk多项式构造无需穷举全部码字即可计算完整重量分布的精确算法。

Result: 给出了C_{m,n}的广义Hamming重量谱的完整确定；证明了C_n的齐次重量枚举器对称当且仅当n为偶数；导出了重量枚举器系数的闭式公式（基于二项式系数与交替卷积）；提出了基于Krawtchouk多项式的精确重量分布计算程序，并通过数值示例验证了公式的正确性。

Conclusion: 本文为二进制单奇偶校验乘积码提供了完整的结构刻画和精确的枚举工具，填补了该码类在重量分布理论方面的空白，其闭式公式和Krawtchouk多项式方法为后续研究长码的重量分析提供了有效途径。

Abstract: We investigate structural and enumerative properties of binary single parity-check product codes. For each $n\geq 2$, $\operatorname{SPC}(n)$ denotes the binary single parity-check code of length $n$, consisting of all binary vectors of length $n$ having even Hamming weight. We determine the generalized Hamming weight hierarchy of the product code $\mathcal{C}_{m,n}=\operatorname{SPC}(m)\otimes\operatorname{SPC}(n)$, whose codewords can be represented as $m\times n$ binary matrices in which every row and every column has even Hamming weight. For the square product $\mathcal{C}_n =\operatorname{SPC}(n)\otimes\operatorname{SPC}(n)$, we also determine the maximum codeword weight and prove that its homogeneous weight enumerator is symmetric if and only if $n$ is even. After characterizing the dual code, we apply the MacWilliams identity in its Walsh--Hadamard formulation to derive an exact closed-form expression for the weight enumerator. By grouping the auxiliary binary vectors according to their Hamming weights, we obtain an explicit formula for each coefficient in terms of binomial coefficients and alternating convolutions. Finally, using Krawtchouk polynomials, we present an exact procedure for computing the full weight distribution without exhaustively enumerating all codewords. Numerical examples illustrate the formulas and verify the resulting computations.

</details>


### [213] [Decoding Algorithms for MDS Array Codes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26483)
*Sara D. Cardell,Gustavo Terra Bastos,Cintya Wink de Oliveira Benedito*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 本文研究了一类由超正则矩阵与非奇异矩阵的Kronecker积在有限域上构造的MDS阵列码的译码过程。针对不同信道模型，利用校验矩阵的特殊结构开发了译码算法：擦除信道下可恢复任意至多n-k个符号擦除模式；q元对称信道下给出了一个和两个符号错误的定位与取值显式过程；并特别分析了超正则矩阵为Vandermonde矩阵时的额外代数结构利用方法，附有不同有限域上的显式示例。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: MDS阵列码具有最优的存储与带宽效率，在分布式存储系统中应用广泛。此前已有工作从Kronecker积构造了一族MDS阵列码，但缺乏系统的译码算法研究，特别是在不同信道模型下的高效译码方案尚未明确，因此需要针对其特殊结构开发相应的译码策略。

Method: 本文利用所构造MDS阵列码校验矩阵的特殊结构（由超正则矩阵与非常异矩阵的Kronecker积构成），分别针对擦除信道和q元对称信道设计译码算法。擦除信道下提出可恢复任意至多n-k个符号擦除模式的算法；q元对称信道下给出单符号和双符号错误的定位与数值求解的显式过程；并在Vandermonde矩阵的特殊情形下利用其额外代数结构简化译码。

Result: 提出了擦除信道下恢复任意至多n-k个符号擦除的完整译码算法；给出了q元对称信道下检测并纠正一个和两个符号错误的显式程序；Vandermonde情形下展示了如何进一步利用代数结构提升译码效率；并提供了不同有限域上的具体数值示例验证所提出方法的正确性。

Conclusion: 本文为该族Kronecker积构造的MDS阵列码建立了完整的译码框架，覆盖了擦除信道与q元对称信道两种主要错误模型，并展示了特定结构（Vandermonde）下译码的进一步优化途径，为该类码的实际应用奠定了基础。

Abstract: We study decoding procedures for a family of MDS array codes previously constructed from the Kronecker product of a superregular matrix and a non-singular matrix over a finite field. By exploiting the particular structure of their parity-check matrices, we develop decoding algorithms for different channel models. For the erasure channel, we provide an algorithm capable of recovering any pattern of up to $n-k$ symbol erasures. For the $q$-ary symmetric channel, we investigate the decoding of one and two symbol errors and give explicit procedures for determining their locations and values. We also consider the particular case in which the superregular matrix is a Vandermonde matrix, showing how its additional algebraic structure can be exploited in the decoding process. Explicit examples over different finite fields are provided to illustrate the proposed procedures.

</details>


### [214] [Sharp Minimax Regret for Infinite-Memory Logistic Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26515)
*Vaneet Aggarwal*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 本文研究了一类特定有限字母表、外生驱动且具有无限输入记忆的信源的在线预测问题。针对带Rademacher输入和可加性衰减系数（受可和包络约束）的自回归型logit模型，作者通过局部化贝叶斯混合方法给出了上界，并通过Toeplitz设计反向构造给出了匹配的下界，证明$\Gamma_T(r)=\sum_j\log(1+n_{T,j}r_j^2)$是该源类在指数和多项式衰减包络下的极小极大累计遗憾阶，分别给出$Θ(\alpha^{-1}\log^2T)$和$Θ(T^{1/(2s)})$的结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在线预测中，有限字母表信源的无限记忆建模是一个核心挑战。已有工作对有限记忆信源有较充分刻画，但对无限记忆、外生驱动的信源，其极小极大遗憾的精确尺度尚不清晰。本文旨在确定这类信源在可和衰减包络下的累计遗憾的紧致界，并区分该问题与一般平稳无限记忆源（profile-only）的差异。

Method: 1) 上界：构造局部化贝叶斯混合预测器，借助滞后分辨谱$\Gamma_T(r)=\sum_j\log(1+n_{T,j}r_j^2)$量化各滞后的贡献，证明对任意可和包络有$\mathcal{R}_T(r)\leq C\Gamma_T(r)$。2) 下界：采用Toeplitz设计（Toeplitz-design）构造对抗性反例，在有限样本维度条件下证明$\mathcal{R}_T(r)\geq c\Gamma_T(r)$，常数仅依赖于固定的衰减参数与logit界。3) 对指数和多项式包络分别导出闭式渐近界。4) 分析了截断记忆（仅保留最近$h$个输入）导致的遗憾代价及其与最坏情况截断剖面之间的非等价性，并引入缩放在线牛顿预测器达到谱上界。

Result: 1) 对任意可和包络，$\mathcal{R}_T(r)\leq C\Gamma_T(r)$成立。2) 在指数与多项式包络下，有限样本维度条件成立时，由Toeplitz设计给出匹配下界$\mathcal{R}_T(r)\geq c\Gamma_T(r)$，从而确定$\Gamma_T(r)$为该源类的极小极大累计遗憾尺度。3) 具体地，$r_j=Ae^{-\alpha j}$时遗憾为$Θ(\alpha^{-1}\log^2T)$；$r_j=Aj^{-s}$（$s>1$）时为$Θ(T^{1/(2s)})$。4) 证明该下界方法特定于外生滞后模型，不适用于一般平稳无限记忆源的profile-only定理。5) 揭示了相同最坏截断剖面可对应多项式级别不同的遗憾，表明有界贝叶斯截断并非充分统计量。

Conclusion: 本文完整刻画了外生滞后驱动无限记忆信源的极小最大遗憾尺度，确立了$\Gamma_T(r)$作为核心量纲的地位。它为指数衰减给出$Θ(\log^2T)$、为多项式衰减给出$Θ(T^{1/(2s)})$的紧致遗憾界，并指明了该结果对外生滞后结构的专门适用性，以及截断记忆在实际预测中的成本非平凡性。缩放在线牛顿法在计算效率与最优性之间取得了平衡。

Abstract: We study online prediction for a specific finite-alphabet, exogenously driven source with infinite input memory. Independent Rademacher inputs $(U_t)$ are observed sequentially, and the next binary mark has logit $\sum_{j=1}^{t}θ_jU_{t+1-j}$, where $\abs{θ_j}\leq r_j$ and $\sum_jr_j\leq B$. Regret is expected cumulative excess log loss. Lag $j$ can affect prediction by scale $r_j$ and enters only $n_{T,j}=T-j+1$ prediction rounds, leading to the lag-resolved spectrum $Γ_T(r)=\sum_{j=1}^{T}\log\!\left(1+n_{T,j}r_j^2\right)$. For every summable envelope, a localized Bayesian mixture proves $\cR_T(r)\leq CΓ_T(r)$. For exponential and polynomial envelopes, under the stated finite-sample dimension condition, a Toeplitz-design converse proves $\cR_T(r)\geq cΓ_T(r)$, with constants allowed to depend on the fixed decay parameters and the logit bound. Thus $Γ_T(r)$ is the minimax cumulative-regret scale for this source class in these canonical regimes, giving $Θ(α^{-1}\log^2T)$ for $r_j=Ae^{-αj}$ and $Θ(T^{1/(2s)})$ for $r_j=Aj^{-s}$, $s>1$. The converse is specific to the exogenous lagged model and is not a profile-only theorem for arbitrary stationary infinite-memory sources. Retaining only the most recent $h$ inputs costs order $\sum_{j>h}n_{T,j}θ_j^2$, yet the same worst-case truncation profile can correspond to polynomially different regret. A scaled online Newton predictor attains the spectrum upper bound.

</details>


### [215] [Spectral Approximation and Ergodic-Capacity Convergence of HMIMO Channels under Spatial-Wavenumber Domain Mismatch](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26802)
*Hangsong Yan,Hong Yang,Shu Sun*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 本文针对具有方形孔径和圆形波数支撑的连续全息MIMO信道，建立了有限维谱逼近和遍历容量收敛的定量结果。由于空间-波数域不匹配导致非可分离的方形-圆盘集中问题，经典PSWF可分离构造无法直接应用。作者将连续算子投影到1D PSWF的张量积子空间上同时保持圆形波数支撑，得到一般非对角但高度稀疏的有限维矩阵，并证明全谱逼近误差由1D PSWF特征值尾部包络控制，在一维截断阈值以上呈超指数衰减。同时建立了连续信道与张量PSWF截断信道之间遍历容量间隙的非渐近上界，该上界继承相同的超指数依赖。最后开发了具有显式径向和角度节点阈值的求积规则用于矩阵求值。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 连续HMIMO信道的有限维谱逼近和容量分析通常依赖于可分离的PSWF构造，但实际物理场景中方形孔径配合圆形波数支撑会导致非可分离的方形-圆盘集中问题，经典方法失效。需要建立在这种不匹配条件下仍能保证谱逼近精度和容量收敛的定量理论框架。

Method: (1) 将连续算子投影到1D PSWF张量积子空间，同时保持圆形波数支撑，得到非对角但稀疏的有限维矩阵；(2) 通过1D PSWF特征值尾部包络控制全谱逼近误差（含保留特征值扰动与残差谱尾）；(3) 推导平坦化2D特征值排序下的特征谱渐近上包络；(4) 建立连续与截断信道间遍历容量间隙的非渐近上界；(5) 开发具有显式径向和角度节点阈值的求积规则。

Result: 全谱逼近误差受1D PSWF特征值尾部包络控制，且在一维截断阈值以上超指数衰减；容量间隙上界继承相同的超指数依赖；数值结果表明基于空间自由度的传统截断可能遗漏性能相关模式，尤其在紧凑孔径场景下。

Conclusion: 该工作为方形孔径-圆形波数支撑的非可分离HMIMO信道提供了严格的理论保障，证明即使失去可分离性，基于1D PSWF的张量积投影仍能实现超指数精度的谱逼近和容量收敛，并揭示了传统空间自由度截断的局限性。

Abstract: We establish quantitative results on finite-dimensional spectral approximation and ergodic-capacity convergence for continuous Holographic Multiple-Input Multiple-Output (HMIMO) channels with square apertures and physically prescribed circular wavenumber support. The resulting spatial-wavenumber domain mismatch leads to a non-separable square-disk concentration problem for which the classical separable construction based on prolate spheroidal wave functions (PSWFs) cannot be directly applied. We project the continuous operator onto a tensor-product subspace of one-dimensional (1D) PSWFs while preserving the circular wavenumber support, yielding a generally non-diagonal but highly sparse finite-dimensional matrix. We show that the whole-spectrum approximation error, accounting for retained-eigenvalue perturbations and the residual spectral tail, remains controlled by a 1D PSWF eigenvalue-tail envelope despite the loss of separability and induced off-diagonal coupling. Beyond an explicit 1D truncation threshold, this error decays super-exponentially. This analysis further yields an asymptotic upper envelope for the eigenspectrum under the flattened two-dimensional eigenvalue ordering. We further establish a non-asymptotic upper bound on the gap between the actual ergodic capacities of the continuous and tensor-PSWF-truncated channels under their respective transmit-covariance optimizations. Combined with the spectral result, this capacity-gap bound inherits the same super-exponential dependence on the truncation order. Finally, quadrature rules with explicit radial and angular node thresholds are developed for evaluating the projected matrix. Numerical results show that conventional truncation based on spatial degrees of freedom can omit performance-relevant modes, particularly for compact apertures.

</details>


### [216] [Minimum Rate For Partially Observable Linear System with Side Information: LQG Plant and Gaussian-Markov Source](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26917)
*Sijie Li,Hyeji Kim*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 本文研究部分可观测线性系统中带边信息的最小传输速率问题，证明了一类线性策略足以优化条件有向信息下界，并证明了标量情况下时变和时不变系统的优化问题是凸的，推广了以往仅考虑全观测、部分观测或全观测带边信息的工作。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在有边信息的部分可观测线性系统中，确定最小传输速率是一个基础性问题。此前工作分别考虑了全观测、部分观测、以及全观测带边信息的情况，但尚未统一处理部分可观测且带边信息的一般情形。作者旨在填补这一空白，并探究线性策略在此类问题中的最优性及优化问题的凸性。

Method: 针对LQG系统和Gaussian-Markov信源，采用条件有向信息作为速率下界的目标度量，证明一类线性政策对于优化该下界是充分的；随后分别对时变与时不变标量系统证明相应的优化问题是凸优化问题。

Result: 证明了线性策略类在优化条件有向信息下界时是充分的；标量情况下，时变和时不变系统的优化问题均为凸问题；数值仿真展示了边信息在部分可观测系统中的实际效用。

Conclusion: 该工作将既有结果统一推广到部分可观测且带边信息的更一般场景，为边信息辅助下部分可观测系统的速率分配问题提供了理论基础和可求解的凸优化框架。

Abstract: This paper studies the minimum rate required for a partially observable linear system with side information. The Linear Quadratic Gaussian(LQG) plant and the Gaussian-Markov source are considered. We show that a class of linear policies is sufficient for optimizing the conditional directed information lower bound. We also show that the resulting optimization problem is convex for the scalar case in both time-varying and time-invariant systems. Our results generalize the past works that consider the case with full or partial observation only, and the case with full observation and side information. Numerical simulations are presented to illustrate the effect of side information for partially observable systems.

</details>


### [217] [Real-Time Reconstruction of Markov Sources over MPR Channels](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27116)
*Pansee S. Elessawy,Nikolaos Pappas*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 本文研究了在具有多包接收（MPR）能力的共享无线信道上，对两个二进制马尔可夫源进行实时重构与远程驱动的采样策略优化问题。作者推导了重构误差（RTE）与驱动误差（CAE）的闭式表达式，证明了独立随机化策略下的最优搜索可归约为有限的一维边界分支搜索，并以联合动作时间共享为基准量化了独立随机化的性能损失。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于碰撞的信道模型往往丢弃同时传输，未充分利用多包接收能力；同时已有工作多关注原始投递率而非面向任务（task-oriented）的通信指标。本文旨在探索MPR在保障重构与驱动任务性能中的实际价值，并回答MPR能力是否足以带来性能提升这一关键问题。

Method: 针对两个传感器观测两个二进制马尔可夫源，在各自采样约束下最小化加权实时重构误差（等价于加权驱动误差）。首先以有效更新概率为变量推导RTE/CAE的闭式表达式；当传感器独立随机化时，MPR诱导的更新率映射呈双线性、优化问题非凸，利用可达更新率域的几何结构将帕累托高效策略搜索归约为有限的一维边界分支搜索（含闭式候选解）；并引入联合传感器动作的时间共享作为基准，证明至多两个帕累托极值模式即可达到最优。

Result: 数值结果表明：MPR能力本身并不充分——只有当并发接收对两个源都可靠时，同时解码才能改善面向任务的性能；否则，避免同时传输的策略同样有效。

Conclusion: MPR在执行任务导向通信中的增益取决于并发接收的可靠性条件；本文提供了独立随机化与时间共享两种方案的完整最优性刻画及性能差距量化，为设计基于共享信道的多源远程重构/驱动系统提供了理论指导。

Abstract: This paper studies the real-time reconstruction and remote actuation of two binary Markov sources over a shared wireless channel with multi-packet reception (MPR). Unlike many existing collision-based formulations that discard simulta- neous transmissions, we exploit MPR and evaluate communica- tion through reconstruction and actuation errors rather than raw delivery rates. We consider two sensors observing the sources and aim to find sampling policies that minimize the weighted real- time reconstruction error (RTE), or equivalently the weighted cost of actuation error (CAE) for the considered binary sources, under per-sensor sampling constraints. We first obtain closed- form expressions for the RTE and CAE in terms of the effective update probabilities. When the sensors randomize independently, the MPR-induced update-rate map becomes bilinear, and the constrained optimization is nonconvex. We exploit the geometry of the achievable update-rate region to show that the search over Pareto-efficient independently randomized policies reduces to a finite set of one-dimensional boundary-branch searches with closed-form candidates. As a benchmark, we allow time sharing among joint sensor actions, and we prove that at most two Pareto- extreme modes are sufficient, and use this benchmark to quantify the loss caused by independent randomization. Numerical results reveal that MPR capability alone is not sufficient; simultaneous decoding improves the task-oriented objective only when con- current reception is reliable for both sources; otherwise, policies that avoid simultaneous transmissions can be equally effective.

</details>


### [218] [Knowledge Distillation Driven Semantic NOMA with GAN Refinement for 6G Robotic Vehicle Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27198)
*Qifei Wang,Zhen Gao,Li Qiao,Ziwei Wan,De Mi,Dapeng Li,Ying Sun*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 本文提出KDG-SemNOMA，一种面向6G机器人车辆上行NOMA网络的语义通信框架，通过ConvNeXt-based DeepJSCC架构、两阶段知识蒸馏和cGAN生成增强模块，在带宽和能量约束下实现高保真视觉感知。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 6G赋能的机器人车辆需要高保真视觉感知，但面临严格的带宽和能量约束。语义通信虽能提供频谱效率优势，但在上行NOMA RV网络中遭受严重干扰，现有方法难以在干扰环境下同时兼顾像素级精度和感知保真度。

Method: 提出KDG-SemNOMA框架，包含三个核心组件：(1) 基于ConvNeXt的DeepJSCC架构，集成增强注意力特征模块实现动态信道自适应；(2) 两阶段知识蒸馏策略，利用正交传输教师模型指导NOMA学生模型进行干扰缓解，不增加推理开销；(3) 信道条件GAN，以第一阶段粗重建和信道状态为条件输入，细化输出为高保真图像并恢复真实纹理。

Result: 在FFHQ-256数据集上的实验表明，KDG-SemNOMA在像素级准确度和感知保真度方面均显著优于现有最先进方法。

Conclusion: KDG-SemNOMA通过知识蒸馏与生成模型增强的有效结合，成功解决了NOMA RV网络中语义通信的干扰问题，实现了鲁棒且绿色的机器人车辆通信，为可持续智能出行提供了可行方案。

Abstract: To achieve sustainable intelligent mobility, 6G-empowered robotic vehicles (RVs) require high-fidelity visual perception under stringent bandwidth and energy constraints. Semantic communication offers a spectral-efficient solution but suffers from severe interference in uplink non-orthogonal multiple access (NOMA) RV networks. To address this, we propose a knowledge distillation-driven and generative models-enhanced NOMA framework for robust and green RV communications, named KDG-SemNOMA. First, we develop a ConvNeXt-based deep joint source-channel coding (DeepJSCC) architecture with an enhanced attention feature (AF) module for dynamic channel adaptation. Second, to mitigate interference without inference overhead, an orthogonal transmission teacher model guides the NOMA student model via a two-stage knowledge distillation strategy. Finally, to address the over-smoothing artifacts of pixel-wise optimization, we introduce a channel-conditional GAN (cGAN). By explicitly taking the Stage-I initial reconstruction and channel states as conditional inputs, this module refines coarse outputs into high-fidelity images with realistic textures. Experiments on FFHQ-256 demonstrate that KDG-SemNOMA significantly outperforms state-of-the-art methods in both pixel-level accuracy and perceptual fidelity.

</details>


### [219] [Deterministic Identification over Additive Gaussian Channels](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27243)
*Jonathan E. W. Huffmann,Holger Boche*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 本文通过引入格理论（lattice theory）的新视角，首次为加性高斯信道（包括加性白高斯信道）建立了确定性识别（deterministic identification）容量的紧致界，填补了该领域长期以来在加性高斯信道下确定性识别容量未知的空白。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代通信系统对数据率、可靠性和功耗提出了严格要求，后香农信息论中的识别通道（identification via channels）范式相比传统信道编码能提供显著更高的识别速率。确定性识别在实现复杂度和通信增益之间提供了良好平衡，是未来通信系统（包括分子通信）的有前景方案。然而，加性高斯信道（尤其AWGN信道）作为最重要的信道模型之一，其确定性识别容量至今未知，这构成了亟需解决的理论空白。

Method: 本文提出了一种观察确定性识别的新视角，并借助格理论（lattice theory）中的数学工具来分析加性高斯信道下的确定性识别问题，通过将识别编码问题与格的结构性质建立联系，从而推导出确定性识别容量的上界和下界。

Result: 针对加性高斯信道（包括最简单的AWGN信道情形），本文建立了确定性识别容量的紧致界（tight bounds），首次给出了该信道模型下确定性识别容量的精确刻画范围。

Conclusion: 通过将格理论应用于确定性识别分析，本文成功解决了加性高斯信道下确定性识别容量长期未知的问题，为该领域提供了新的理论工具和容量结果，对未来通信系统的设计（包括分子通信等）具有重要指导意义。

Abstract: Modern communication systems impose strict demands on data rate,
  reliability, and power efficiency. In this context, emerging communication
  paradigms such as identification via channels have become an important topic
  in post-Shannon information theory, offering the potential for substantially
  higher identification rates than in conventional channel coding.Deterministic
  identification is particularly interesting for specialized communication
  scenarios because it provides a balance between implementation complexity and
  the communication gains due to higher identification rates. It is therefore a
  promising communication scheme for future communication systems, including
  molecular communication systems.
  Additive Gaussian channels, particularly the additive white Gaussian channel,
  are among the most important channel models for analyzing the performance of
  communication systems in information and communication theory. This importance
  stems from both their mathematical tractability and their ubiquitous
  appearance in practical applications.
  To date the deterministic identification capacity for additive Gaussian
  channels remains unknown even for the simplest case of the additive white
  Gaussian channel. In this paper, we establish tight bounds on the
  deterministic identification capacity of additive Gaussian channels by
  introducing a new perspective on deterministic identification. To this end, we
  apply results from lattice theory to obtain new capacity results.

</details>


<div id='cs.AI'></div>

# cs.AI [[Back]](#toc)

### [220] [EduRiskX: A Neuro-Symbolic Framework with F-Logic Reasoning for Early Academic Risk Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26107)
*Yu Fu,Yongqi Kang,Yong Zhao,Rongfang Bie*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出EduRiskX，一种神经符号框架，用于在线教育中的学生学业风险预测。该框架结合基于时间Transformer的预测器与F-Logic符号推理，通过逻辑回归融合机制整合神经风险概率与符号置信度得分。在OULAD数据集上采用严格的80/10/10学生级划分，实现了0.900的准确率和0.894的F1分数，平均早期检测周数为9.32周，检测率达94.30%，优于PatchTST、iTransformer、LSTM和CNN等基线模型，同时通过F-Logic规则提供可解释的预测依据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有在线教育学业风险预测模型存在两个核心问题：早期检测能力有限，无法及时干预；缺乏可解释性，形成"黑箱"信任危机，阻碍了模型在实际教学场景中的落地应用。

Method: EduRiskX采用神经符号混合架构：1）神经组件使用时间Transformer建模纵向学生活动序列，结合时间注意力、类别加权损失和动态周截断；2）符号组件构建基于F-Logic的规则库，以Engagement Theory和Student Integration Model等教育理论为基础，仅从训练数据中提取规则，模拟人类教育者的诊断逻辑；3）通过基于逻辑回归的融合机制，学习神经风险概率与符号置信度得分的相对贡献。

Result: 在OULAD数据集上，EduRiskX在第38周（学期末）达到0.900的准确率和0.894的F1分数，平均早期检测周数为9.32，检测率为94.30%。在相同条件下，相比PatchTST、iTransformer、LSTM和CNN等模型，EduRiskX取得了更高的召回率和更早的风险识别。此外，F-Logic模块能提供结构化的基于规则的解释，将预测与可观察的行为模式和教学理论关联起来。

Conclusion: EduRiskX通过融合深度学习的时间序列建模能力与符号推理的可解释性，在保持甚至提升预测性能的同时解决了现有模型的"黑箱"问题，为在线教育学业风险预测提供了兼具高性能与高可信度的解决方案。

Abstract: Predicting students' academic risk in online education is crucial for enabling timely interventions that can improve retention and learning outcomes. However, existing models often suffer from limited early detection capability and insufficient interpretability, leading to a "black-box" trust crisis that hinders their adoption in real-world pedagogical settings. To address these challenges, we propose EduRiskX, a neuro-symbolic framework that integrates a temporal Transformer-based predictor with F-Logic symbolic reasoning. The neural component models longitudinal student activity sequences using temporal attention, class-weighted loss, and dynamic weekly truncation. Acting as a data-driven expert system, an F-Logic rule base -- grounded in established educational theories (Engagement Theory and Student Integration Model) to mimic the diagnostic logic of human educators -- is constructed exclusively from the training data. The neural risk probability and the symbolic confidence score are then combined through a logistic regression-based fusion mechanism that learns the relative contribution of each signal. Experiments on the Open University Learning Analytics Dataset (OULAD) using a strict 80/10/10 student-level split show that EduRiskX achieves an accuracy of 0.900 and an F1-score of 0.894 at the end of the semester (Week 38), with an average early detection week of 9.32 and a detection rate of 94.30 percent. Compared with state-of-the-art time-series models (PatchTST, iTransformer) and common deep learning baselines (LSTM, CNN), EduRiskX yields improved recall and earlier risk identification under identical conditions. Beyond predictive performance, the F-Logic module provides structured rule-based explanations linking predictions to observable behavioral patterns and educational theories.

</details>


### [221] [Standalone LLM and a Pre-specified Agentic Pipeline for Explaining ICU Mortality Predictions: a Feasibility Study on the eICU Demo Dataset](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26109)
*Di Zhu,Chen Xie,Haoyun Zhang,Zihan Wei,Ziwei Wang,Jiazhao Shi,Ziyu Wang,Qiyang Xie*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本研究评估了用于ICU死亡风险预测解释的独立LLM与四步agentic流水线的对比，发现agentic方法在指南依据性和患者特异性细节上更优，但在SHAP归因一致性上低于独立LLM，建议将两者结合使用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器学习模型能准确预测ICU死亡率，但特征归因方法（如SHAP）难以提供临床叙事，限制了床旁应用。LLM有望弥合这一鸿沟，而多步agentic流水线通过将数据解释、指南检查和最终解释分离，可能提供更可靠的临床解释。该研究旨在评估agentic分解是否优于独立LLM解释生成。

Method: 研究基于eICU Demo数据集（2,353例ICU住院，死亡率8.1%），使用XGBoost模型（AUROC 0.855）作为基础预测器。在38例分层解释子集上比较独立LLM与四步agentic流水线的输出，并在与SHAP审查子集重叠的14例上评估SHAP归因一致性、方向一致性、指南依据性、数值特异性和可理解性等指标。

Result: agentic流水线未产生结果泄漏（独立LLM有1例），在指南依据性（0.762 vs 0.143）、数值特异性（0.236 vs 0.143）和可理解性（0.700 vs 0.671）上优于独立LLM；但独立LLM在SHAP对齐（Jaccard 0.171 vs 0.077）和方向一致性（92.9% vs 78.6%）上更高。

Conclusion: agentic分解可提升解释的安全性相关依据和患者特异性细节，但在高风险风险解释场景中仍需与归因校验手段结合使用，以弥补其在归因一致性上的不足。

Abstract: Machine-learning models can predict ICU mortality accurately, but feature-attribution methods alone rarely provide the clinical narrative needed for bedside use. Large language models (LLMs) may bridge this gap, and multi-step agentic pipelines are a plausible extension because they separate data interpretation, guideline checking, and final explanation. This revised feasibility study preserves the original standalone-versus-agentic comparison while making the main clinical findings more explicit. Using the retained local eICU Demo artifact set (2,353 ICU stays; 8.1\% mortality), XGBoost achieved an AUROC of 0.855 (95\% CI 0.796--0.906) and an AUPRC of 0.332 (95\% CI 0.217--0.494). On a stratified 38-case explanation subset, the standalone LLM produced 1 explanation with explicit outcome leakage, whereas the four-step agentic pipeline produced none. Among the 14 cases that overlapped with the SHAP review subset, the standalone LLM showed higher SHAP alignment (mean Jaccard 0.171 versus 0.077) and higher direction consistency (92.9\% versus 78.6\%), while the agentic pipeline showed higher guideline grounding (0.762 versus 0.143), higher value specificity (0.236 versus 0.143), and slightly higher plausibility (0.700 versus 0.671). Clinically, the results suggest that agentic decomposition may improve safety-relevant grounding and patient-specific detail, but it should be paired with attribution-based checks before use in high-stakes risk explanation.

</details>


### [222] [Large Models for Battery Prognostics and Health Management: A Review and Future Roadmap](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26111)
*Jiale Liu,Huan Wang,Weicheng Wang,Rong Zhu,Qiqi Wang,Min Xie*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文首次系统综述了大型模型（LMs）在电池健康与寿命管理（BPHM）中的应用，探讨了基于Transformer架构和自监督预训练的大型模型如何克服传统物理模型和深度学习方法在计算效率、跨域泛化、数据依赖和可解释性方面的瓶颈，并提出了面向数据、智能、可信度和部署四个方面的发展路线图。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统BPHM方法（物理模型和任务中心深度学习）在计算效率、参数化、跨域泛化、对大量退化数据集的依赖以及模型可解释性方面存在长期瓶颈。随着大型模型（LMs）的快速发展，迫切需要系统梳理其在BPHM中的应用现状与潜力，以推动下一代电池管理系统的发展。

Method: 本文采用综述研究方法，首先阐述了支撑大型模型的基础技术（Transformer架构、自监督学习、大规模多模态数据集、PEFT技术），然后从四个关键维度对现有研究进行分类梳理：缓解数据稀缺、增强泛化与鲁棒性、融合领域知识实现可解释性、实现系统级自动化。

Result: 综述表明大型模型在BPHM中展现出显著潜力，能有效缓解数据稀缺、提升跨域泛化能力、增强模型可解释性并支持系统级自动化。但同时，在数据可访问性、智能验证、可信赖性及部署可行性方面仍存在重大挑战。

Conclusion: 作者提出了面向未来的研究路线图，包括构建协作式数据生态系统、验证面向工业应用的智能、通过物理信息设计增强可信赖性、以及实现高效的设备端部署。该综述为研究人员和实践者理解和推进大型模型驱动的BPHM提供了系统性框架和核心洞见。

Abstract: Battery Prognostics and Health Management (BPHM) is critical for ensuring the safe, reliable, and cost-effective operation of batteries across electric vehicles, grid storage, and consumer electronics. Conventional BPHM approaches, including physics-based models and task-centric deep learning methods, face challenges in computational efficiency and parameterization, cross-domain generalization, dependence on extensive labeled run-to-failure data, and model interpretability. Recent Large Models (LMs), built upon Transformer architectures and self-supervised pre-training, offer a transformative new paradigm to overcome these long-standing bottlenecks. This review provides the first comprehensive survey of LM applications in BPHM, systematically examining how these models address challenges in the field. We begin by elucidating the foundational technologies enabling LMs, including Transformer architectures, self-supervised learning, large-scale multimodal datasets, and PEFT techniques. We then categorize recent progress along four critical dimensions: mitigating data scarcity, enhancing generalization and robustness, integrating domain knowledge for interpretability, and enabling system-level automation. Despite promising results, significant challenges remain across data accessibility, intelligence validation, trustworthiness, and deployment feasibility. To guide future research, we propose a roadmap focused on building collaborative data ecosystems, validating intelligence for industrial applications, enhancing trustworthiness with physics-informed designs, and enabling efficient on-device deployment. This review establishes a systematic approach to understand and advance LM-driven BPHM, providing researchers and practitioners with essential insights for developing next-generation battery management systems capable of safe, reliable, and autonomous operation throughout battery lifecycles.

</details>


### [223] [PICasso: An AI-Enabled Design Framework for Autonomous Optimization of Silicon Photonic Devices](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26113)
*Deepak Vungarala,Deniz Najafi,Abdulrahman Aljoudi,Zahra Ghanaatian,Navid Khoshavi,Gourav Datta,Arman Roohi,Mahdi Nikdast,Shaahin Angizi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出PICasso，一个AI辅助框架，将自然语言规范自动转化为可制造的光子集成电路（PIC）版图，并通过仿真引导优化提升性能。同时引入PIC-Set基准（36个参数化PIC设计任务），实验表明结构Spec@3达92.7%，功能Spec@3达52%，插入损耗从4.98dB降至3.25dB。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 光子集成电路设计传统上依赖手工GUI流程，繁琐且耗时。尽管大语言模型（LLM）在电路生成方面展现出潜力，但直接使用LLM生成PIC版图存在结构不合法、缺乏物理验证和仿真反馈等问题，无法产出可制造的布局。需要一个将LLM能力与领域约束、物理验证和仿真优化相结合的自动化框架。

Method: PICasso采用NL->YAML->GDS的结构化生成流水线，包含PDK感知知识注入、自动布局与布线、DRC/LVS验证以及基于SAX的光子仿真。同时提出PIC-Set基准（36个参数化PIC设计任务），涵盖核心光子原语和多组件电路，并使用统一的评估协议（包括结构/功能Spec@k、优化效率和鲁棒性指标）对多个SOTA LLM进行基准测试。

Result: PICasso相比vanilla LLM生成显著提升了端到端规范满足度：高复杂度电路上结构Spec@3达92.7%，功能Spec@3达52%。通过仿真引导优化，平均插入损耗从4.98dB降低至3.25dB（改善1.74dB），且运行时间与手动GUI工作流相当。

Conclusion: 结构化领域约束、物理验证和仿真反馈能够将LLM从脆弱的网表生成器转变为实用的PIC设计代理，能够产出具有竞争力的可制造布局。PICasso展示了AI驱动的光子设计自动化在效率和可制造性方面的显著优势。

Abstract: We present PICasso, an AI-assisted framework for automated synthesis, verification, and optimization of photonic integrated circuits (PICs) from natural-language specifications. PICasso couples a structured NL -> YAML -> GDS generation pipeline with PDK aware knowledge injection, automated placement and routing, DRC/LVS validation, and SAX-based photonic simulation. To systematically evaluate AI-driven photonic design, we introduce PIC-Set, a benchmark of 36 parameterized PIC design tasks spanning core photonic primitives and multi-component circuits. Using PIC-Set, we benchmark several state-of-the-art Large Language Models (LLMs) under a unified evaluation protocol, including new metrics such as structural and functional $Spec@k$, optimization efficiency, and robustness under perturbations. Across the benchmark, PICasso significantly improves end-to-end specification satisfaction compared to vanilla LLM generation. Structural $Spec@3$ reaches up to 92.7% and functional $Spec@3$ up to 52% on high-complexity circuits. In addition, PICasso consistently reduces circuit insertion loss, lowering the mean loss from 4.98 dB to 3.25 dB (1.74 dB improvement) through simulation-guided optimization. These results demonstrate that structured domain constraints, physical verification, and simulation feedback transform LLMs from brittle netlist generators into practical PIC design agents capable of producing manufacturable layouts with competitive runtimes relative to manual GUI-based workflows.

</details>


### [224] [CIFQA: A Deterministic Tool-Grounded Multi-Agent LLM Framework for Financial Query Answering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26114)
*Kunjesh Parekh,Anil Kumar Tiwari,Divya Saxena*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出CIFQA，一个确定性工具驱动的多智能体LLM框架，用于计算密集型金融问答。该框架将语言理解与数值执行分离，通过专门智能体处理查询解释、路由、参数提取、计算规划和响应生成，并使用确定性Python工具执行金融计算。在定期存款查询基准上达到95.54%的计算密集型查询准确率，显著优于直接LLM基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型在自然语言任务上表现出色，但在解决多步金融计算问题时往往产生数值错误却看似合理的答案。金融问答涉及结构化利率、时间条件、数值公式和基于规则的约束，需要精确推理，传统LLM方法无法可靠满足这一需求。

Method: CIFQA框架采用多智能体架构，将语言理解与数值执行解耦。它分配专门的智能体分别负责查询解释、路由、参数提取、计算规划和响应生成，同时采用确定性Python工具执行金融计算和规则应用。论文将该框架实例化用于定期存款（fixed deposit）查询问答。

Result: 在精心整理的定期存款查询基准上，CIFQA在计算密集型查询上达到95.54%的准确率，总体准确率为90.87%，大幅优于即使提供了完整公式、利率表和基准指令的直接LLM基线。消融实验表明确定性组件（精确利率查找、期限计算、滚动年度调整、提前支取逻辑）是性能的关键贡献者。17B开源骨干模型在CIFQA框架内优于使用相同金融信息的更大规模前沿模型。

Conclusion: CIFQA证明了架构设计比模型规模更能决定数值可靠性。虽然仅在定期存款查询上进行了评估，但该框架为计算密集型金融推理任务提供了可泛化的解决方案。

Abstract: Calculation-intensive financial question answering requires exact reasoning over structured rates, temporal conditions, numerical formulas, and rule-based constraints. Although Large Language Models (LLMs) perform strongly on natural language tasks, they often produce numerically incorrect yet plausible answers when solving multi-step financial calculations. To address this limitation, we introduce CIFQA (Calculation-Intensive Financial Query Answering), a deterministic tool-grounded multi-agent LLM framework for financial question answering. CIFQA separates language understanding from numerical execution by assigning specialized agents to query interpretation, routing, parameter extraction, computation planning, and response generation, while deterministic Python-based tools perform financial calculations and rule application. We instantiate CIFQA for fixed deposit query answering and evaluate it on a curated benchmark of fixed deposit queries. CIFQA achieves 95.54% accuracy on calculation-intensive queries and 90.87% overall accuracy, substantially outperforming direct LLM baselines even when provided with complete formulas, rate cards, and benchmark instructions. Ablation studies show that deterministic components such as exact rate lookup, tenure computation, rolling-year adjustment, and premature-withdrawal logic are critical contributors to performance. Notably, a 17B open-source backbone operating within CIFQA outperforms substantially larger frontier models evaluated with the same financial information, demonstrating that architectural design is a more important determinant of numerical reliability than model scale. While evaluated on fixed deposit queries, CIFQA provides a generalizable framework for calculation-intensive financial reasoning tasks.

</details>


### [225] [The Artificial Experimentalist: Discovery and Control of Self-Organizing Phenomena with Autotelic Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26116)
*Marko Cvjetko,Benedikt Hartl,Michael Levin,Clément Moulin-Frier,Pierre-Yves Oudeyer*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出CARL框架，将自决强化学习（autotelic RL）与Lenia连续元胞自动机相结合，实现闭环干预复杂系统。CARL能够自主发现稳定孤子、控制已有孤子的运动方向，并支持人类通过高层指令在实时环境中引导孤子穿过迷宫。训练后的智能体在多种目标、更新规则和初始状态下展现出零样本泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有探索元胞自动机等复杂系统的方法多为开环方式：设定初始条件后执行完整模拟并观察结果，无法在执行过程中进行干预。这限制了研究者对涌现现象的主动控制和发现能力。

Method: 基于自决强化学习构建闭环框架：智能体自主采样多样化目标，学习目标条件化策略，通过最小化、局部的扰动干预复杂系统运行过程。在Lenia连续元胞自动机上实例化该系统，命名为CARL。

Result: CARL在多种Lenia更新规则下以高于启发式基线的方法发现稳定孤子；学会用少量干预控制已有孤子的运动方向；人类可通过高层方向指令让训练好的智能体在迷宫中实时引导孤子。训练后的策略在分布外条件下实现零样本泛化。

Conclusion: 研究展示了通往人工实验智能体的路径——这类智能体能够自主或借助人类指导，在复杂系统中发现和控制涌现现象，为复杂系统的闭环探索提供了新范式。

Abstract: Existing methods for exploring cellular automata and other complex systems mostly operate in open loop: they set initial conditions, execute a full simulation, and observe the outcome, without intervening during execution. We introduce a closed-loop framework based on autotelic reinforcement learning, in which an agent autonomously samples diverse goals and learns a goal-conditioned policy to intervene in a complex system through minimal, local perturbations. We instantiate this framework on Lenia, a continuous cellular automaton known for life-like self-organizing patterns, in an agentic system we call CARL, and demonstrate three capabilities. First, CARL discovers stable solitons across a wide range of Lenia update rules at a higher rate than heuristic baselines. Second, it learns to steer the movement direction of existing solitons with few interventions, showing that CARL can control self-organizing patterns, not only create them. Third, humans can use trained agents to guide solitons through maze environments in real time by specifying high-level directional commands that the agent translates into low-level interventions. Trained across diverse goals, update rules, and random initial states, the agents acquire policies that generalize zero-shot to various out-of-distribution conditions. These results suggest a path toward artificial experimentalist agents that, autonomously or with human guidance, discover and control emergent phenomena in complex systems.

</details>


### [226] [The Accuracy-Efficiency Paradox Quantifying Net Energy Loss in on-Device Energy Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26134)
*Jaeik Jeong,Tai-Yeon Ku,Wan-Ki Park*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文揭示了能源预测中的"精度-效率悖论"：高精度模型在边缘设备上可能因推理能耗和电池老化反而造成净能量亏损，并提出基于总拥有成本（TCO）的框架来最小化净能量损失。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统能源预测只追求精度最大化，但忽略了边缘AI推理能耗和电池老化带来的能量损耗，在任务关键型边缘环境（如军事系统）中，高精度模型可能适得其反，造成净能量赤字。

Method: 提出一个总拥有成本（TCO）框架，将推理能量消耗和电池老化统一视为能量损失形式，因为电池退化是对系统未来储电能力的物理耗散，并通过该框架评估复杂架构在热敏感边缘环境中的能量收益与损失。

Result: 实验表明，在热敏感边缘环境中，复杂架构通过更高精度节省的能量往往被其高运行强度带来的总能量损失所抵消，验证了精度-效率悖论的存在。

Conclusion: 能源预测不仅要考虑预测精度，还需将在边缘设备上的推理能耗和电池老化纳入优化目标，以实现真正的净能量效益。

Abstract: Energy forecasting aims to maximize accuracy to ensure energy efficiency by reducing energy waste, an objective that applies equally to on-device forecasting for mission-critical edge environments, including military systems. However, this paper identifies the Accuracy-Efficiency Paradox: high-precision energy forecasting models can ironically trigger a net energy deficit. This stems from both edge AI's inference energy consumption and battery aging. We propose a Total Cost of Ownership (TCO) framework for energy forecasting, designed to minimize net energy loss. This framework treats not only inference energy consumption but also battery aging as a unified form of energy loss, as degradation represents a physical dissipation of the system's future energy-carrying capacity. We demonstrate that in thermally sensitive edge environments, energy saved by the superior precision of complex architectures is often outweighed by the total energy lost through their high operational intensity.

</details>


### [227] [LLMs for Academic Workflows: An Evaluation of Literature Reviews Generated with Short and Long Context Windows of LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26145)
*Muhammad Ali Chaudhry,Xinyuan Hao,Haifa Alwahaby*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本研究评估了LLM在短、长上下文设置下生成的文献综述质量，发现AI生成的综述需要人工监督才能达到学术出版标准，长上下文虽能纳入更广泛信息但也加剧了内容重复、关键工作遗漏和描述性有余而综合性不足等问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究上下文窗口大小对AI生成文献综述质量的影响，以及AI在辅助文献综述写作中应扮演的角色。

Method: 基于Semantic Scholar和Arxiv的研究来源，生成20篇AI文献综述，由两位研究人员在15个维度上进行评估。

Result: AI生成的综述需要人工监督才能达到学术出版标准；上下文窗口增大时，LLM能纳入更广泛信息并保持长输入连贯性，但同时加剧了内容重复、关键工作遗漏以及重描述轻综合的问题。

Conclusion: AI生成的综述可作为基础性概述，但必须经领域专家批判性地评估和精炼；未来应探索集成其他LLM和微调模型，采用人机混合的方法来解决已识别的局限性。

Abstract: Our research focuses on evaluating literature reviews generated in short and long context settings of large language models (LLMs) to investigate the impact of context window on the quality of AI-generated literature reviews and the role of AI in supporting literature review writing. Twenty AI-generated literature reviews based on research sources from Semantic Scholar and Arxiv were evaluated by two researchers across 15 dimensions. Our findings reveal that AI-generated literature reviews require human oversight to meet academic publishing standards. As context windows increase, LLMs can incorporate broader information and maintain coherence across longer inputs, but they also exacerbate issues such as content repetition, omission of critical work, and a tendency towards descriptiveness over synthesis. Our work shows that AI-generated reviews can provide foundational overviews, but their output must be critically evaluated and refined by domain experts. Future research should consider integrating other LLMs and fine-tuned models in different domains with hybrid approaches that combine human expertise with AI capabilities to address the limitations identified in this study.

</details>


### [228] [AI agents in Algorithmic Electricity Markets: On the Emergence of Tacit Collusion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26896)
*Jakub Seredyński,Georgios Tsaousoglou*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究电力市场中基于学习算法（多智能体强化学习）的竞价策略是否可能导致隐性合谋，结果表明智能体确实能在未被指示合谋的情况下学会维持超竞争性结果，构成隐性合谋的现实威胁。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 电力市场参与者日益采用基于学习的智能体进行竞价，市场正变得算法化。其他领域的算法市场证据表明纯独立学习即可产生隐性合谋。加之电力市场是寡头垄断且参与者反复互动，结构上易于出现非竞争性行为，因此有必要验证电力市场中可能存在隐性合谋的假设。

Method: 将策略性竞价建模为不完全公共监测下的重复博弈，采用多智能体强化学习来模拟参与者的涌现行为；提出一套超越（与纳什均衡利润对比的）多维标准来评估行为是否构成隐性合谋。

Result: 实验结果表明隐性合谋的危险在电力市场中是真实存在的：存在某些案例中，尽管智能体从未被指示合谋，它们确实学会了维持支持隐性合谋指标的超竞争性结果。

Conclusion: 自主学习算法在电力市场竞价中可能涌现隐性合谋行为，这种风险是实际且需要警惕的，监管部门应关注算法化市场中的反竞争潜在威胁。

Abstract: As electricity market participants increasingly adopt learning-based agents for their bidding strategies, electricity markets are becoming algorithmic. Evidence from algorithmic markets in other domains shows that tacit collusion can arise purely through independent learning. Moreover, electricity markets are typically oligopolistic and feature repeated interaction among a small number of participants, making them structurally susceptible to non-competitive behavior. In the face of these observations, this paper investigates the hypothesis that tacit collusion may emerge in electricity markets where participants' actions are controlled by autonomous learning-based algorithms. We model strategic bidding as a repeated game with imperfect public monitoring, and model the participants' emergent behavior using multi-agent reinforcement learning. We propose a multi-dimensional set of criteria (going beyond profit comparisons against Nash equilibria) to assess whether the resulting behavior constitutes tacit collusion. Our experimental results showcase that such a danger is realistic for electricity markets: there are cases where agents do learn to sustain supra-competitive outcomes that are supportive of tacit collusion indicators, even though the agents were never instructed to collude.

</details>


### [229] [Methodological and Conceptual Framework for 5D Multi-Table Analysis: A Unified Approach for Complex Data Reuse](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26149)
*Edouard Lansiaux,Hugo Kazzi,Aurélien Loison,Slim Hammadi,Emmanuel Chazard*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出关系超图Transformer（RHT），一种将关系数据库表示为超图的统一架构，通过五维嵌入（PentE）和稀疏关系注意力实现与平均关系度成正比的复杂度，在Synthea电子健康记录数据集上的多标签SNOMED CT编码预测任务中取得了较好的语义嵌入一致性，同时保持计算可扩展性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多表学习在医疗等领域仍是重大挑战，关系数据包含大数据量、高维变量、高基数类别特征、复杂的表间依赖及重复时间观测等多种复杂性来源，现有方法难以同时处理这些挑战。

Method: RHT将关系数据库表示为超图，学习五维嵌入（PentE），并执行稀疏关系注意力机制，其复杂度与平均关系度成正比而非实体数量的平方。作者还提供了开源参考实现和形式化复杂度推导。

Result: 在Synthea合成EHR数据集上的多标签SNOMED CT编码预测中，RHT相比表格、关系和时间图基线方法产生更语义连贯的嵌入，同时保持计算可扩展性。消融实验量化了各架构组件的贡献。XGBoost在罕见编码召回率上表现最佳，RHT在嵌入语义一致性上最强。

Conclusion: RHT是一种可扩展且语义表现良好的关系数据学习统一架构，但在罕见类别预测上仍不及XGBoost。未来将在MIMIC-IV上进行临床验证。

Abstract: Multi-table learning remains a major challenge in machine learning for healthcare and other complex information systems. Relational data combine several sources of complexity, including large data volume, high-dimensional variables, high-cardinality categorical features, complex inter-table dependencies, and repeated temporal observations. We introduce the Relational Hypergraph Transformer (RHT), a unified architecture that represents relational databases as hypergraphs, learns pentadimensional embeddings (PentE), and performs sparse relational attention with complexity proportional to the average relational degree rather than the square of the number of entities. We formally define the architecture, derive the complexity of its attention mechanism, and provide an open-source reference implementation. We evaluate RHT on the public Synthea synthetic electronic health record dataset using multi-label prediction of SNOMED CT condition codes per encounter, a task characterized by high categorical cardinality and long-tailed label distributions. Comparisons with tabular, relational, and temporal graph baselines show that RHT produces more semantically coherent embeddings while remaining computationally scalable. In this benchmark, the highest rare-code recall is achieved by XGBoost, whereas RHT attains the strongest embedding semantic coherence. We also report ablation studies quantifying the contribution of each architectural component. Clinical validation on MIMIC-IV is planned following PhysioNet credentialing. Source code and experimental protocols are provided in the accompanying repository.

</details>


### [230] [Leveraging Large Language Models for Systematic Literature Review of Disease Spread Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26150)
*Orhan Yagizer Cinar,Timur Emre Ozkose,Emma Von Hoene,Amira Roess,Taylor Anderson,Hamdi Kavak*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本研究开发了一个LLM流水线，用于从536篇基于代理建模（ABM）论文中自动提取模型相关信息，并与人工系统文献综述（SLR）结果对比。GPT-4.1和GPT-5.0的论文级准确率分别约为77.95%和81.67%，字段级准确率在32.40%-100.00%之间。研究发现LLM间的一致性是输出质量的潜在指标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型语言模型的最新进展为自动化研究流程（包括系统文献综述SLR）提供了新机遇，本研究旨在探索LLM在从大量论文中提取建模相关信息方面的自动化潜力，并评估其相对于人工SLR的性能表现。

Method: 开发了一个LLM流水线用于从536篇同行评审的基于代理建模论文中提取模型相关信息，并与人工执行的系统文献综述结果进行比较。对比了GPT-4.1和GPT-5.0两个模型的表现，分析了论文级和字段级的准确率，并考察了LLM之间的一致性作为输出质量的指标。

Result: GPT-4.1的论文级准确率约为77.95%，GPT-5.0约为81.67%。字段级准确率范围为32.40%-100.00%，复杂或主观性较强的字段可靠性较低。LLM间一致性可能指示输出质量：低一致性可能提示幻觉，而高一致性但低准确率可能反映人工数据集中存在噪声或错误。

Conclusion: 该研究为提示工程提供了实用见解，突出了在建模与仿真领域使用LLM进行全规模系统文献综述的潜力与局限性，并提出LLM间一致性可作为输出质量的有效参考指标。

Abstract: Recent advancements in Large Language Models (LLMs) have created new opportunities to streamline and potentially automate many research processes, including systematic literature reviews (SLRs). This study reports an LLM pipeline development for extracting model-relevant information from 536 peer-reviewed agent-based modeling papers. We compare the results with those of a human-conducted SLR. Our results show paper-level accuracies of approximately 77.95% for GPT-4.1 and 81.67% for GPT-5.0. Field-level accuracy ranges from 32.40% to 100.00%, with more complex or subjective fields performing less reliably. Importantly, we find that agreement between LLMs is a potential indicator of output quality: low agreement may signal hallucinations, whereas high agreement combined with low accuracy may point to noise or errors in the human dataset. Overall, our study provides practical insights into prompt development and highlights both the potential and limitations of using LLMs for full-scale SLRs in the modeling and simulation domain.

</details>


### [231] [Explainable Artificial Intelligence for Customer Churn Prediction in Telecommunications: A Framework for CRM Integration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26151)
*Sandeep Gaddamwar*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文针对电信客户流失预测中"高精度模型不透明、无法落地CRM"的问题，在IBM Telco基准上基准测试了四种分类器（Logistic回归、随机森林、XGBoost、LightGBM），并结合SHAP与LIME提供全局与实例级可解释性分析，最终提出一个四层CRM集成架构，将风险评分与归因向量转化为分层细分、留存行动模板与再训练反馈闭环，预计可将最高风险五分位客户的流失率降低3.3–5.3个百分点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 电信行业客户流失每月约1.9%，造成数十亿美元年收入损失。虽然预测模型能准确识别有流失风险的客户，但高性能的集成和非线性架构因缺乏可解释性而被一线CRM工作流排除在外——仅靠概率分数无法支撑留存专员设计个性化干预方案。

Method: 在IBM Telco客户流失基准（7,043条记录、19个特征、26.5%流失率，训练集经SMOTE平衡至50%）上，基准测试Logistic回归、随机森林、XGBoost和LightGBM四种分类器；使用5折交叉验证；提供全局SHAP排序（识别tenure、总费用、月付合同为主导信号）和实例级SHAP与LIME分解；构建四层CRM集成架构，将风险评分与归因向量转化为分层客户细分、特征到留存行动模板的映射，并将活动结果路由至再训练反馈回路。

Result: Logistic回归取得最强AUC-ROC（0.8411），LightGBM取得最高准确率（78.42%）；四种模型AUC差距仅0.011（0.831–0.841），5折交叉验证表明领先模型在统计上基本持平。定向最高风险五分位客户预计可将总流失率降低3.3–5.3个百分点，每个活动周期可节省约19.9万–31.9万美元。

Conclusion: 模型性能差异在统计上不显著（AUC均在0.84附近），因此选择模型时应优先考虑可解释性与可操作性而非微小的性能增量。将可解释AI（SHAP/LIME）与分层CRM干预流程深度集成，能够在保持高模型性能的同时实现个性化留存干预，从而转化为可量化的商业价值。

Abstract: Subscriber attrition is a costly, persistent challenge for telecommunications providers, with monthly churn of roughly 1.9% in mature markets eroding billions in revenue annually. Predictive models can flag at-risk customers accurately, yet they are routinely excluded from frontline CRM workflows because high-performing ensemble and non-linear architectures are opaque: a retention specialist cannot design a personalised intervention from a probability score alone, without knowing why a subscriber is at risk. This paper addresses that gap. We benchmark four classifiers--Logistic Regression, Random Forest, XGBoost, and LightGBM--on the IBM Telco Customer Churn benchmark (7,043 records; 19 features; 26.5% churn, balanced to 50% via SMOTE on the training partition only). Logistic Regression attains the strongest AUC-ROC (0.8411) and LightGBM the highest accuracy (78.42%); all four fall within a 0.011 AUC band (0.831--0.841), and 5-fold cross-validation confirms the leading models are effectively tied. Explanations are delivered at two granularities: a global SHAP ranking identifying tenure, total charges, and month-to-month contract as the dominant churn signals, and instance-level SHAP and LIME decompositions that expose the drivers behind each prediction. Building on these outputs, we introduce a four-layer CRM integration architecture that converts risk scores and attribution vectors into tiered segmentation, maps top features to structured retention-action templates, and routes campaign outcomes into a retraining feedback loop. Targeting the highest-risk quintile is projected to cut overall churn by 3.3--5.3 percentage points, preserving an estimated $199K--$319K per campaign cycle.

</details>


### [232] [EEG-to-Report: An Annotation and Feature-Text Framework for Training Language Models on Clinical EEG](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26153)
*Xuan-The Tran,Le Trung Kien Nguyen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文介绍EEG-to-Report，一个基于浏览器的EEG标注与特征文本框架，将常规EEG审阅与AI就绪数据集构建相结合，通过多模态标注、特征提取和自动报告模块为训练EEG-语言模型提供对齐的feature-text监督数据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 临床EEG报告目前仍以手动为主且耗时，现有EEG软件生态无法生成训练现代语言模型所需的结构化EEG-文本监督数据，大多数工具箱仅关注可视化或预处理，缺乏生成高质量AI数据集的完整工作流支持。

Method: 框架包含四个核心组件：1) 多格式EEG数据导入与通道标准化；2) 交互式查看器与多模态标注层（结合键入文本和语音转录）；3) 特征提取引擎，为每个标注片段计算标准化光谱、时域、熵、Hjorth、连接性和尖峰相关描述符，并存储于便携JSON schema；4) 自动报告模块，集成卷积神经网络集成与大语言模型草拟临床叙述供神经科医生审阅。

Result: 通过试点标注，展示了EEG-to-Report能简化标注工作流并生成可编辑的草稿报告，产生对齐的feature-text对用于监督多模态EEG-语言模型。

Conclusion: 该框架为自动化EEG报告系统提供了可复用的基础，弥合了临床EEG审阅与AI模型训练数据构建之间的鸿沟。

Abstract: Clinical electroencephalography (EEG) reporting remains largely manual and time-consuming, and current EEG software ecosystems do not produce the structured EEG-text supervision needed for training modern language models. Most toolboxes focus on visualization or preprocessing, providing limited support for workflows that generate high-quality datasets for AI. We introduce EEG-to-Report, a browser-based annotation and feature-text framework that links routine EEG review with the construction of AI-ready datasets. The framework integrates multi-format EEG ingestion, channel standardization, and an interactive viewer with a multimodal annotation layer that combines typed text and transcribed voice notes. For each annotated segment, a feature extraction engine computes a standardized set of spectral, temporal, entropy, Hjorth, connectivity, and spike-related descriptors, stored alongside clinical descriptions in a portable JSON schema. This yields aligned feature-text pairs designed to supervise multimodal EEG-language models. The framework also includes an auto-report module that couples an ensemble of convolutional networks with a large language model to draft clinical narratives for neurologist review. Using pilot annotations, we describe how EEG-to-Report streamlines annotation workflows and produces editable draft reports, providing a reusable foundation for automated EEG reporting systems.

</details>


### [233] [Selection Bias Correction in Retail Intelligence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26156)
*Spandan Ghose Chowdhury*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本研究通过400次蒙特卡洛模拟，在四种数据生成场景下比较了逆概率加权（IPW）与分层法在零售长尾商品通货膨胀估计中的选择性偏差校正效果。结果表明，在多数场景下分层法表现更优（优势可达116倍），而IPW仅在多项式平滑关系场景下略胜一筹。即使在完美结构知识的oracle IPW设定下，IPW在阶跃函数场景中误差仍高达6.06pp，远逊于分层法的0.008pp，根本原因在于Positivity Assumption（正性假设）的严重违背——当选择概率差异极大（90% vs 1%）时，加权方法超出了其理论适用范围。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 零售情报通常聚焦于高流量的热门商品，可能因忽略"长尾"利基商品而导致经济指标的估计偏差。研究旨在量化这一选择偏差对通货膨胀估计的影响，并系统地比较不同校正方法的稳健性。

Method: 采用400次蒙特卡洛复现，涵盖四种数据生成场景：对齐的阶跃函数、平滑梯度、错位断点、以及多项式关系。对比了五种不同设定的逆概率加权（IPW）方法与不同分层数量的分层法，并在特定场景中引入oracle IPW作为上界基准。

Result: 分层法在四种场景中的三种表现优于IPW，中位误差保持在0.04个百分点以下，即使分界与总体断点错位时仍具有116倍的优势。IPW结合样条倾向模型在多项式平滑关系下胜出（中位误差0.007pp vs 0.013pp）。oracle IPW在阶跃函数场景中误差为6.06pp，远差于分层法的0.008pp。

Conclusion: 在零售长尾分布且存在严重正性假设违背的条件下，分层法是更安全的工程选择。加权方法在极端选择概率差异（90% vs 1%）下超出其理论设计范围，其性能瓶颈源于因果推断的基本假设（Positivity Assumption）被违反，而非方法本身的局限。

Abstract: Retail intelligence often relies on monitoring popular, high-velocity products, potentially biasing economic indicators by ignoring the "long tail" of niche items. This simulation study investigates selection bias in inflation estimation and compares correction methods across diverse data-generating processes. Through 400 Monte Carlo replications spanning four scenarios--aligned step functions, smooth gradients, misaligned breaks, and polynomial relationships--we test the robustness of Inverse Probability Weighting (IPW) with five specifications against stratification with varying strata counts. Our findings reveal fundamental limits of weighting methods in retail long-tail contexts: stratification achieves superior performance in three of four scenarios, maintaining sub-0.04pp median error even when boundaries deliberately misalign with population breaks (116x advantage over IPW). However, IPW with spline propensity models wins under smooth polynomial relationships (median error 0.007pp vs. 0.013pp), demonstrating context-dependency. Critically, even an oracle IPW specification with perfect structural knowledge achieves 6.06pp error compared to stratification's 0.008pp in step-function scenarios. This reflects violation of the Positivity Assumption--a fundamental causal inference requirement--rather than IPW methodological inferiority. When selection probabilities differ dramatically (90% vs. 1%), weighting methods operate outside their theoretical design envelope. These results demonstrate that stratification provides a safer engineering choice in retail long-tail distributions with severe positivity violations.

</details>


### [234] [GROUND: Reducing Hallucinations in LLM-Based Enterprise Analytics Through Governed Semantic Definitions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26157)
*Aravind Sasidharan Pillai*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出GROUND框架，通过治理语义层约束LLM生成的文本到SQL分析，确保指标、维度、连接路径、过滤器和行级安全均受管控，并在验证失败时重试或弃答。100题基准测试中，GROUND是唯一在六个评估类别中均无幻觉的系统，且行级安全在所有模型上零违规。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 企业数据仓库上的自然语言分析在生产环境中受到幻觉指标、无效连接、错误粒度、不安全数据访问和不支持的解释等问题的严重限制。现有文本到SQL系统仅基于数据库schema或检索文档进行生成，缺乏对企业报表所需的受治理业务语义（如经批准的指标、维度、连接路径、过滤器和行级安全）的支持。

Method: GROUND框架包含三个核心环节：1) 提供经批准的语义层定义；2) 将用户意图绑定到受治理的指标和维度；3) 在执行前依据schema、指标、连接、粒度、过滤器、安全和成本规则验证生成的SQL，违规时进行重试或弃答。语义层包括治理后的指标定义和访问策略（如行级安全），确保生成过程受业务语义约束。

Result: 在100题合成企业报表基准中，GROUND是唯一在所有六个评估类别中均无测量幻觉的系统，而未治理系统在多个问题上违反行级安全。仅有精确指标定义但无访问策略的语义-only条件仍会泄露数据。在真实NHTSA车辆安全数据上，GROUND的强制保证（尤其过滤器和行级安全）在所有四个模型上零违规，但依赖判断的行为（如拒绝未定义指标）仍可能犯错。

Conclusion: 治理不能仅靠指标精度替代——语义层必须包含强制性的访问策略和验证机制。GROUND证明了通过受治理语义层+规则验证的方式可以有效消除幻觉和安全违规，但依赖于模型判断的任务（如拒答）仍有改进空间。

Abstract: Natural-language analytics over enterprise data warehouses is increasingly important, but production use is limited by hallucinated metrics, invalid joins, wrong grain, unsafe data access, and unsupported explanations. Existing text-to-SQL systems often ground generation in database schemas or retrieved documentation, while enterprise reporting also requires governed business semantics: approved metrics, dimensions, join paths, filters, and row-level security. This paper introduces GROUND, Governed Retrieval Over Unified Normalized Definitions, a framework that constrains LLM-generated analytics to a governed semantic layer. GROUND supplies approved definitions, binds user intent to governed metrics and dimensions, and validates generated SQL against schema, metric, join, grain, filter, security, and cost rules before execution. On violations, it retries or abstains.
  In a 100-question synthetic enterprise-reporting benchmark, GROUND is compared with direct schema-only text-to-SQL, schema-RAG, and semantic-only grounding under one shared model. GROUND is the only system free of measured hallucinations across all six evaluated categories, while ungoverned systems violate row-level security on many questions. A semantic-only condition with exact metric definitions but no access policy still leaks data, showing that governance cannot be replaced by metric fidelity alone. The findings are replicated on real U.S. NHTSA vehicle-safety data with independent hand-authored gold and tested on an adversarial set across four models from three providers. GROUND's enforced guarantees, especially filters and row-level security, hold with zero violations on every model, while judgment-dependent behaviors such as refusing undefined metrics remain fallible.

</details>


### [235] [SAREF-based Ontology for Distributed AI Workflows across the Edge-Fog-Cloud Continuum](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26160)
*Viorica Rozina Chifu,Tudor Cioara,Vasile Ofrim,Liana Toderean,Ionut Anghel,Laura Daniele,Cornelis Bouter*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出一个符合SAREF标准的本体，用于在边缘-雾-云连续体上表示分布式AI工作流，通过扩展SAREF4SYST本体增加AI流水线、可执行AI作业、计算资源、部署约束和通信关系等概念，实现语义互操作性、自动推理和资源感知的AI应用编排。实验证明在异构环境下部署成功率达90-100%，平均编排决策时间低于80ms。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有语义模型对分布式AI工作流及其在异构边缘、雾和云环境中的执行支持有限，导致AI流程和资源使用不兼容的语义表示，影响了互操作性、编排和复用能力。

Method: 扩展SAREF4SYST本体，引入AI流水线、可执行AI作业、计算资源、部署约束和通信关系等新概念，建立统一语义模型覆盖AI工作流与异构计算基础设施，并通过智能电网能源服务编排场景进行概念验证评估。

Result: 所有能力问题均通过SPARQL查询和语义推理成功验证；部署成功率达90-100%，平均编排决策时间低于80ms，证明该方法在异构边缘-雾-云环境中确保语义互操作性的有效性。

Conclusion: 所提出的SAREF兼容本体能够有效支持分布式AI工作流在异构环境中的部署、执行推理和工作负载自适应，同时完全对齐ETSI SAREF生态系统，为分布式AI编排提供语义互操作性保障。

Abstract: Nowadays semantic models provide limited support for representing distributed AI workflows and their execution across heterogeneous edge, fog, and cloud environments. Therefore, AI processes and resources are often described using incompatible semantic representations, affecting the interoperability, orchestration, and reuse. To address these challenges, this paper proposes a SAREF-compliant ontology for representing distributed AI workflows across the edge-fog-cloud continuum. We extend the SAREF4SYST ontology with concepts for modeling AI pipelines, executable AI jobs, computational resources, deployment constraints, and communication relationships, providing a unified semantic model of both AI workflows and heterogeneous computing infrastructures. The ontology enables semantic interoperability, automated reasoning, and resource-aware orchestration of distributed AI applications while remaining fully aligned with the ETSI SAREF ecosystem. The ontology is evaluated using proof-of-concept smart grid energy services orchestration scenarios and validated using competency questions showing its ability to support AI workflow deployment, execution reasoning, and workload adaptation across heterogeneous edge, fog, and cloud environments. All competency questions were successfully validated using SPARQL querying and semantic reasoning. Experimental results demonstrate deployment success rates of 90-100% with average orchestration decision times below 80 ms across heterogeneous edge-fog-cloud environments, highlighting its effectiveness on ensuring semantic interoperability for distributed AI orchestration.

</details>


### [236] [A Safety-Gated Multimodal AI Backend for Mental-Health Support: Hierarchical State Representation, Conservative Risk Fusion, and Controlled Generation in Anian](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26162)
*Lei Wang,Xiao Wang,Lei Li*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出Anian，一个面向围产期心理健康支持与正念干预路由的安全门控多模态AI后端系统。该系统的模块化管线将生成式AI置于结构化状态表示、保守风险融合和响应门控之后，在高风险时阻断自由生成并替换为固定安全内容和人工支持指引。内部原型评估显示L1情绪分类Micro-F1为0.9604、L2心理社会构念为0.9144、L4路由为0.9742，L3风险引擎在233个样本的压力测试中高风险召回率达1.0000。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 安全关键的心理健康支持系统必须能够区分何时支持性对话是合适的、何时应阻断自由形式生成，以防止AI生成的回复对处于心理危机中的用户造成伤害。现有生成式AI在心理健康领域缺乏系统性的安全门控机制。

Method: Anian采用模块化管线，将生成式AI置于生成式AI下游的四层结构化状态表示（L1情绪状态、L2心理社会构念、L3安全风险、L4干预路由）之后。系统将用户文本或语音ASR转录映射到这四层，通过最高风险优先规则（S_fusion = max(S_local, S_external)）融合本地文本/规则安全证据与外部语音证据。在中等或高风险时阻断AI生成回复和TTS输出，替换为固定安全内容和人工支持提示。使用约858,295条规范化记录进行弱标签和规则驱动框架下的内部评估。

Result: 内部原型评估中，L1情绪分类Micro-F1为0.9604，L2心理社会构念为0.9144，L4路由为0.9742。在233个样本的安全压力测试中，L3规则引擎在预设场景下实现高风险召回率1.0000。

Conclusion: Anian的标签框架与门控逻辑在内部具备可行性，但该结果不能确立临床有效性、诊断准确性、真实世界安全性或有效性。未来需要专家评审和真实世界验证。

Abstract: Safety-critical mental-health support systems must distinguish when supportive conversation is appropriate from when free-form generation should be blocked. This paper presents Anian, a safety-gated multimodal AI backend for perinatal mental-health support and mindfulness-intervention routing. Anian is not intended to diagnose psychiatric conditions or replace clinical care or crisis intervention. Its modular pipeline places generative AI downstream of structured state representation, conservative risk fusion, and response gating. User text or voice-derived ASR transcripts are mapped into four linked layers: L1 emotion states, L2 psychosocial constructs, L3 safety risk, and L4 intervention routes. Local text- and rule-based safety evidence is fused with external voice-derived evidence using a highest-risk-priority rule, S_fusion = max(S_local, S_external). At moderate or high fused risk, ordinary AI-generated responses and text-to-speech delivery are blocked and replaced by fixed safety content and prompts for human support. An internal prototype evaluation used approximately 858,295 normalized records from public emotion, dialogue, mental-health-related, and Chinese dialogue corpora within a weak-label and rule-derived framework. Micro-F1 scores were 0.9604 for L1 emotion classification, 0.9144 for L2 psychosocial constructs, and 0.9742 for L4 routing. In a controlled safety stress test of 233 samples, the L3 rule engine achieved high-risk recall of 1.0000 within predefined scenarios. These findings support the internal feasibility of the label framework and gating logic but do not establish clinical validity, diagnostic accuracy, real-world safety, or effectiveness. We report the architecture, ontology, safety-fusion mechanism, prototype evaluation, error-analysis plan, and roadmap for expert-reviewed and real-world validation.

</details>
