On the Observation side, a context-isolated Action Probe exposes action-inducing semantics and persistently records action-origin provenance across steps as a review signal; on the execution side, actual tool calls are authorized only against the user objective and audited evidence from authorized successful executions, while satisfying goal, execution-chain, and argument-level support. To preserve this separation across multi-step execution, SARA applies No-History-Promotion to prevent historical recurrence from laundering action origins into execution authority. Across AgentDojo and AgentDyn, SARA limits ASR to no more than \(0.63\%\) across four primary evaluation settings while maintaining competitive task utility, and consistently reduces ASR across additional Agent backbones.

</details>


### [302] [Thomson: Continual Learning of Frontier Models for SovereignAI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27147)
*Shengzhuang Chen,Jerrod Parker,Yejin Bang,Andrew M. Bean,Nabeel Seedat,Stefan Winzeck,Daniil Glazko,Jannik Zgraggen,Fangyi Yu,Scott Arnott,Dietrich Trautmann,Luca Ciuffreda,Guglielmo Bonifazi,Davide Romano,Bradley Bell,Kirsty Fielding,Daniele Giofrè,Tom Zielund,Ipshita Chatterjee,Sneha Murthy Ghantasala,Manpreet Nanreh,John Scoville,Maciej Sakowicz,Wassim Seifeddine,Lukas Thede,Jonathan Richard Schwarz*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文论证了通过持续学习（Continual Learning）在开放权重模型基础上，广泛机构而非仅限少数资金充裕的巨头即可实现前沿模型性能。作者展示了Thomson模型——一个面向高权重专业工作的通用前沿模型，在代理任务、安全、法律、税务、多语言及深度研究等方面与近期前沿模型竞争，且呈现独特的π形能力提升模式，几乎完全消除了窄域适配中常见的遗忘问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 前沿模型的开发通常被认为只有少数大型资金雄厚的玩家才能实现，造成开发者与用户之间的信息、经济和权力不对称。虽然"主权AI"（SovereignAI）的概念已被提出，但缺乏在多样化资金条件下短期内实现的具体指导。本文旨在论证在充足预算之外，通过持续学习即可实现前沿性能，使更大范围的机构能够拥有SovereignAI栈（模型、工具基础设施、价值观与数据隐私）。

Method: 本文提出利用现代中期与后期训练栈（mid- & post-training stack）在开放权重模型上进行持续学习，而非仅依赖小规模微调、提示工程或冻结模型的工具增强等受限方法。每个阶段引入保持可塑性与稳定性的保护机制，对参数施加最少且高影响的干预，从而降低成本并避免遗忘问题。作者以此训练出Thomson模型，并在高权重专业任务上进行增强训练。

Result: Thomson模型在代理任务、安全、法律、税务及多语言能力以及大规模深度研究等广泛基准上表现出与近期前沿模型相当的性能。评估显示其呈现独特的π形模式：在广泛能力（包括未明确目标的能力）上均有显著提升，同时几乎完全消除了窄域领域适配中常见的灾难性遗忘问题。计算与人员预算显著低于普遍预期，但仍获得相当于跨越多个模型世代的性能增益。

Conclusion: 前沿性能并非少数大型实验室的专属特权，通过持续学习和开放权重模型，更广泛的机构可以以相对较低的预算获得前沿模型能力，并拥有SovereignAI栈的绝大部分。该方法使模型所有权（包括模型权重、工具基础设施、数据隐私与价值对齐）对更多参与者变得可行，有助于缓解AI开发中的权力与信息不对称。

Abstract: The development of frontier models is commonly perceived to be the exclusive remit of a small number of heavily funded players, creating an information, economic and power asymmetry between developers and the diverse user base of modern AI. Recent public discourse acknowledges this concern, calling for SovereignAI (an organisation's capability to independently build, deploy and govern AI use), but offers little concrete advice on how this can be achieved in the short term under a diversity of funding settings. We argue that frontier performance is achievable by a wide range of institutions through Continual Learning on readily available open-weight models. Unlike limited approaches such as small-scale fine-tuning, prompt engineering, or tool-augmentation of a frozen model, our approach exploits a modern mid- & post-training stack while introducing safeguards that preserve both plasticity and stability at each stage, making the minimal number of high-impact interventions on the parameters. This yields gains comparable to those typically seen across multiple successive model generations, at compute and personnel budgets substantially lower than commonly thought, making ownership of large parts of the SovereignAI stack (model, tool infrastructure, values & data privacy) viable for far more actors. We demonstrate this with Thomson, a general-purpose frontier model trained with an enhanced focus on high-stakes professional work. Thomson performs competitively with recent frontier models across agentic tasks, safety, legal, tax & multilingualism, and large-scale Deep Research. Evaluations show a distinctive $π$-shaped pattern: distinct improvements across a wide range of capabilities, including those not explicitly targeted, while almost completely eliminating the forgetting problem common to narrow domain adaptation.

</details>


### [303] [BPMN4CAI: A BPMN Extension for Modeling Dynamic Conversational AI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27149)
*Björn-Lennart Eger,Daniel Rose,Barbara Dinter*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出BPMN4CAI，一个基于设计科学研究方法的标准合规BPMN扩展框架，用于在业务流程中表示动态、上下文敏感的对话式AI交互，并通过案例研究验证其应用价值。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 对话式AI系统在数字业务流程中日益重要，但现有的BPMN标准在表示动态、上下文敏感的交互方面存在方法论和实践上的研究空白，需要一种标准合规的建模方法来弥合这一差距。

Method: 采用设计科学研究方法（DSR）系统性地扩展现有BPMN元素，并纳入专门组件，构建BPMN4CAI扩展框架；通过案例研究对其适用性和相关性进行演示和评估。

Result: BPMN4CAI扩展框架在案例研究中被证明能够促进业务流程中的自适应决策过程、稳健的上下文管理以及透明的对话式AI交互。

Conclusion: BPMN4CAI作为标准合规的BPMN扩展，有效解决了对话式AI在业务流程建模中的表示问题，为相关领域的实践提供了方法论支持。

Abstract: Conversational AI systems, such as chatbots and virtual assistants, are becoming increasingly important to digital business processes. However, the established Business Process Model and Notation (BPMN) standard faces challenges when representing dynamic, context-sensitive interactions. This paper addresses this methodological and practical research gap by developing a standard-compliant BPMN extension (BPMN4CAI). Using Design Science Research methodology, this paper develops an approach that systematically extends existing BPMN elements and incorporates specialized components. The applicability and relevance of the BPMN4CAI framework are demonstrated and evaluated through a case study. The results show that the BPMN4CAI extension facilitates adaptive decision-making processes, robust context management, and transparent interactions for Conversational AI within business processes.

</details>


### [304] [Calibrated Enough to Know, Not Calibrated to Act: Fabricated Evidence Makes LLM Agents Commit to the Unknowable](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27167)
*Pranav Aggarwal*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 研究发现，面对专业外观的市场面板时，LLM智能体对不可预测问题的方向性判断承诺率显著上升（从6.5%升至54.0%），且即使面板数据完全捏造也会产生类似效果，说明触发自信行动的关键是信息的"包装权威"而非信息本身。模型并非无能（对可回答问题近乎全对）、并非信念问题（概率估计几乎不变）、也并非缺乏判断力（90%能识别不可知问题却仍会行动），真正失败的是"行动/不行动"的门控环节。该门控可通过监督微调修复（540个合成样本即可将承诺率降至0%并跨域迁移），但该门控具有上下文脆弱性——刚性输出格式会破坏门控效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索LLM智能体面对专业呈现的不可预测问题时，为何会做出方向性承诺，以及这种过度自信的根源究竟在于信息、信念、判断力还是行动门控机制。

Method: 在12个前沿模型上，比较模型面对裸问题和附加专业市场面板时的承诺率差异；通过完全捏造面板数据对照实验隔离信息真实性的作用；同时测量模型概率估计、问题可知性分类能力；最后对3B模型进行540个合成案例（骰子、硬币、罐子、计时器）的监督微调，测试门控可训练性与跨域迁移，并检验不同响应格式下的门控稳定性。

Result: 承诺率从6.5%升至54.0%；捏造面板仍将承诺从24.5%提升至36.8%，与真实数据37.6%统计上无差异；可回答问题上模型几乎总是正确回答；概率估计几乎不随证据梯度变化；模型90%能识别不可知问题但仍在0.4%的情况行动；微调后原问题承诺率降至0.0%并迁移到三个未见域；但刚性格式下门控失效。

Conclusion: LLM过度承诺的根源是"行动/不行动"门控环节的失败，而非能力、信念或判断力缺陷；该门控可训练且能跨域泛化，但对响应格式高度敏感；部署时需同时考虑其可训练性和上下文脆弱性两方面特性。

Abstract: An LLM agent shown a professional-looking market panel commits to a directional call on a provably unpredictable question far more often than one asked the bare question: across 12 frontier models, commitment rises from 6.5% to 54.0% as evidence is escalated. It commits just as readily when every number on the panel is invented: fabricating the entire display, so nothing the model can see is true except the question itself, still lifts commitment from 24.5% to 36.8%, statistically indistinguishable from the 37.6% produced by genuine market data. What unlocks confident action is not information but the authority of its packaging. The failure is narrow and locatable. Incapacity is not the answer: on matched answerable questions attached to the same panels, the same models answer essentially always, at near-perfect accuracy. Nor is it belief - stated probabilities barely move across the gradient that swings action by 48 points, and score worse than a climatological baseline. Missing judgment isn't it either: asked to classify a question's knowability before acting, models call it irreducible 90% of the time and then commit on just 0.4% of those. The act/don't-act gate is what fails, and the effect is concentrated in a few models rather than universal. Because the gate is separable, it can be trained. Supervised fine-tuning of a 3B model on 540 synthetic cases, predominantly dice, coins, jars and timers, drives commitment to 0.0% on the original cases and transfers to three unseen domains. It does not survive everything: the gate holds exactly when the response format leaves room to reason, and rigid formats that remove that room leave the model confident and wrong on questions it otherwise answers correctly. The gate is trainable and context-fragile, and deployment needs both halves of that sentence.

</details>


### [305] [What Makes Good Agentic Data? An ACE Lens on Data Generation for LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27260)
*Xingshan Zeng,Zishan Xu,Boju Zhang,Yuzhou Wu,Lingzhi Wang,Jianghao Lin,Liangyou Li,Yasheng Wang,Lifeng Shang,Xin Jiang,Weinan Zhang,Yong Yu,Qun Liu,Weiwen Liu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个用于LLM智能体交互数据生成的两层分析框架：将智能体数据表示为因子化对象(E,q,τ,v)，并通过Accuracy-Complexity-divErsity (ACE)视角将其形式化为受约束的分布设计问题，系统梳理现有工作并揭示从执行锚定准确性、学习者相对复杂度到超越表面多样性演进的趋势。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有智能体数据生成研究分散于多个领域，以领域为中心的组织方式和不一致的评估方法往往掩盖了共通的生成机制，同时将候选构建与验证选择混为一谈。这导致难以系统理解和比较不同方法，也缺乏统一的框架来指导智能体数据生成的设计与发展。

Method: 本文提出两层框架：(1) 将智能体数据表示为共同因子化对象(E,q,τ,v)，其中E为环境规范、q为任务信号、τ为交互实现、v为可选验证器，并按主锚点和依赖结构组织生成范式；(2) 通过ACE视角将生成形式化为受约束的分布设计，其中准确性定义可行支持域，复杂度将学习质量置于声明学习者能力与执行配置的相对位置，多样性控制数据覆盖与冗余。

Result: 通过该框架梳理了先前的验证方法、难度构建与校准、以及行为覆盖扩展工作，揭示了文献中向执行锚定准确性、学习者相对复杂度和超越表面变化或数据集规模多样性的转变趋势，并探讨了面向扩展、数据源、训练范式与自适应学习的更广泛方向。

Conclusion: 智能体数据生成的核心挑战不在于简单生成更多数据，而在于随着智能体与环境演化，持续分配有效、信息丰富且非冗余的经验。该框架为统一理解和指导智能体数据生成提供了解析基础。

Abstract: LLM agents increasingly rely on generated interaction data to learn how to interact with external environments. Agentic data generation must maintain consistency among environments, tasks, interactions, and success signals while producing experience that is useful rather than merely abundant. Existing work spans many agent domains, but domain-centered organization and heterogeneous evaluation often obscure common generation mechanisms and conflate candidate construction with verification and selection. This work develops a two-level framework for the field. First, we represent agentic data as a common factorized object $(E,q,τ,v)$, comprising an environment specification, task signal, interaction realization, and optional verifier. We organize generation paradigms by their primary anchor and dependency structure. Second, we formulate generation as constrained distribution design through the Accuracy-Complexity-divErsity (ACE) lens. Accuracy establishes the feasible support of grounded and internally consistent data. Within this support, Complexity places learning mass relative to the capability of a declared learner and execution configuration, while divErsity controls coverage and redundancy of data. Using this framework, we explore how prior work verifies generated experience, constructs and calibrates difficulty, and expands behavioral coverage. The literature reveals a shift toward execution-grounded accuracy, learner-relative complexity, and diversity beyond surface variation or dataset size. We further discuss broader directions and emerging trends in agentic data generation through the ACE lens, including their implications for scaling, data sources, training regimes and adaptive learning. Overall, the central challenge is not simply to generate more data, but to continually allocate valid, informative, and non-redundant experience as agents and environments evolve.

</details>


### [306] [Naive Prompt Optimization: Rethinking the Need for Complex Prompt Search](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27266)
*Yuan Chang,Xiaoqi Chen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出Naive Prompt Optimization (NPO)，一种轻量级单谱系提示优化方法，通过教师模型配合rollout反馈迭代修订提示词。实验表明NPO在更少rollout下即可达到或超越复杂方法GEPA的性能，且优势随教师模型增强而扩大。在交互式游戏中与GEPA表现相当，但GRPO在某些任务上更优。NPO优化的提示词也能迁移到其他同类学生模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 加速递归自我改进（RSI）中智能体跨任务的高效提升是核心问题，提示优化被证明能在降低计算成本的同时取得与微调相当的增益。然而，近期研究者倾向于设计不必要的复杂提示优化器，作者旨在回归简单，验证轻量级单谱系方法能否匹敌复杂搜索过程。

Method: NPO采用单谱系（single-lineage）迭代框架：使用教师模型基于rollout反馈逐步修订提示词，既不引入多谱系竞争、也不依赖复杂搜索策略，是一种线性的、计算开销极低的提示优化方法。

Result: NPO在更少rollout下达到或超过GEPA性能；教师模型越强，优势越明显；在交互式游戏中与GEPA大体相当，但GRPO在部分不适合提示优化的任务上表现更好；NPO优化后的提示词可原样迁移至其他学生模型，尤其是同族模型间。

Conclusion: 初步结果表明，简单线性的提示优化可以匹敌显著更复杂精巧的搜索方法。更强的教师推理能力可部分替代优化器侧的搜索复杂度，提示优化领域存在对复杂方法过度设计的倾向。

Abstract: Efficiently improving autonomous agents across diverse tasks is central to accelerating recursive self-improvement (RSI) in agentic AI, with prompt optimization emerging as a promising approach capable of delivering performance gains comparable to those achieved by fine-tuning model weights, while reducing computational costs in both optimization and serving. However, recent developments increasingly favor unnecessarily complex prompt optimizers. We introduce Naive Prompt Optimization (NPO), a lightweight single-lineage method that iteratively revises prompts using a teacher model with rollout feedback. NPO achieves comparable or better performance than GEPA with fewer rollouts, and its advantage increases with stronger teacher models, suggesting that stronger teacher reasoning can partially substitute for optimizer-side search complexity. In interactive games, NPO remains broadly competitive with GEPA, while GRPO performs better on some tasks less amenable to prompt optimization. We also show that NPO-optimized prompts elicit similar performance improvements when applied verbatim to other student models, especially across models within the same family. Overall, our preliminary results show that simple, linear prompt optimization can rival substantially more sophisticated and complex search procedures.

</details>


### [307] [BrailleBench: Investigating Multi-Criteria Braille Comprehension in Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27268)
*Jinghan Zhang,Fengran Mo,Zhiyu Chen,Xiaoyan Han,Kunpeng Liu,Chang-Tien Lu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出BrailleBench基准，用于评估大规模语言模型（LLMs）对盲文（Braille）的理解能力，涵盖数学、常识和多跳问答等任务，涵盖英语及盲文1级和2级。评估发现LLMs在印刷英语与盲文能力之间存在持续差距，且盲文理解与表达不对称。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管LLMs在知识获取和计算辅助方面具有强大能力，但现有AI系统对于盲人和聋盲用户是否足够包容尚不清楚。盲文中的指示符、缩写和数字表示对模型理解提出独特要求，而目前缺乏系统性的基准来评估LLMs在盲文理解上的表现。

Method: 构建BrailleBench基准：对齐5,570个来自五个数据集的实例，涵盖数学、常识和多跳问答；设计不同配置以评估系统理解盲文内容、用盲文表达答案以及端到端盲文交互能力；通过确定性、专家审核的流水线构建，使用自创建的盲文工具包，不依赖LLM生成数据。评估六个代表性LLM。

Result: 结果显示LLMs在印刷英语能力与盲文可访问性之间存在持续差距；盲文理解与表达存在不对称性，其中2级盲文在输入侧尤其脆弱（相比1级），完全盲文请求会进一步降低性能。

Conclusion: 实验观察为未来盲文AI系统的发展提供了有价值的指导，BrailleBench的所有相关资源已公开供后续研究使用。

Abstract: Although Large language models (LLMs) mediate access to knowledge and computational assistance, their capabilities should benefit vulnerable groups in the same way. However, it is unclear whether existing AI systems are inclusive enough for blind and deafblind users to access the same functionality through Braille, whose indicators, contractions, and digital representations introduce distinct requirements for model comprehension. To this end, we introduce BrailleBench, a benchmark for evaluating LLMs in Braille comprehension from different Criteria. BrailleBench aligns 5,570 instances from five datasets, including mathematics, commonsense, and multi-hop question answering across English and Braille Grades 1 and 2. Different configurations are designed to understand whether the systems can comprehend Braille-authored content, express answers in Braille, and complete end-to-end Braille interaction. To ensure the quality and prevent evaluation bias, the benchmark is built through a deterministic, expert-reviewed pipeline via a self-created Braille Toolkit without using any data instances generated by LLMs. We evaluate six representative LLMs from various aspects. The results reveal a persistent gap between print-English capability and Braille accessibility. Braille understanding and expression are asymmetric, where Grade 2 is especially fragile on the input side compared to Grade 1, and fully Braille requests further reduce performance. The experimental observations provide valuable guidance for the development of future Braille AI systems. All related resources in BrailleBench are publicly available for future research.

</details>


### [308] [LLMs Can Design Near-Optimal OR Algorithms](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27296)
*Jackie Baek*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究大规模语言模型（LLM）能否为库存控制、排队网络控制和品类优化等运筹学（OR）问题设计有效算法。最强的模型gpt-5.6-sol在几乎所有评估实例上匹配或超越了现有最佳方法，即使在仅给定问题类别描述和参数范围的二级使用场景下也成立，说明前沿LLM可作为运筹学算法设计的严肃实证基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统上算法设计依赖专家的领域知识和大量人工调优。本文希望探究一个根本性问题：LLM是否具备为结构化的运筹学问题自动设计有效算法的能力，以及这种能力在多大程度上可以减少人工输入。

Method: 在三个运筹学问题上进行实证评估：库存控制、排队网络控制和品类优化。设计了两个使用层级：一级使用中模型直接针对单个实例输出解；二级使用中模型仅根据问题类别描述和参数范围生成算法，之后将该算法应用于未见过的评估实例。人工输入被最小化——仅提供一个未调优的问题描述提示词，模型在固定计算预算的Python沙箱环境中工作。

Result: 最强的测试模型gpt-5.6-sol在几乎所有评估实例上匹配或超越了现有最佳方法，包括在二级使用场景下算法在评估前就已固定的情况。同时发现模型性能在相隔不到八个月发布的模型中显著提升，表明该能力正在快速演进。

Conclusion: 对于所研究的良好规范的运筹学问题，单次未调优的LLM查询即可产生与专业方法相竞争的算法。前沿LLM可以作为运筹学算法设计的严肃实证基线，且该能力的发展速度值得关注。

Abstract: We ask whether large language models (LLMs) can design effective algorithms for well-specified operations research (OR) problems. We study inventory control, queueing network control, and assortment optimization. We evaluate two levels of LLM use: at level 1, the model receives one problem instance and returns a solution for that instance; at level 2, it receives only the problem class description and broad parameter ranges, and returns an algorithm that maps instance parameters to solutions. Human input is minimal: we give one untuned prompt that describes the problem, and the model has access to a Python sandbox tool with a fixed compute budget.
  The strongest model we test, gpt-5.6-sol, matches or outperforms the best existing method on almost all evaluated instances. This holds even at level 2, where the returned algorithm is fixed before seeing the evaluation instances. Performance also improves sharply across models released less than eight months apart, suggesting that this capability is moving quickly. Thus, for the well-specified operations problems we study, a single untuned LLM query can already produce algorithms competitive with specialized methods. These results suggest that frontier LLMs can be a serious empirical baseline for algorithm design in well-specified OR problems.

</details>


### [309] [Verify Smarter, Evolve Further: Efficient Harness Evolution through Behavior-Aware Verification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27311)
*Jinghan Xu,Yikai Zhang,Aili Chen,Weiyuan Li,Jiaqing Liang,Deqing Yang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出HarnessLens，一个预算感知的自动化智能体框架（harness）演化框架，通过联合探索任务空间与用户可配置组件、从执行轨迹中推导候选修改、并以可归因证据门控对每个候选进行行为相关任务的验证，在三个智能体框架和四个基准上实现7.6-13.6%的平均留出性能提升，同时显著降低评估预算消耗。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 智能体框架（harness）决定了语言模型智能体如何使用指令、工具和运行时组件，但调整这些框架需要昂贵的验证成本。现有的propose-and-verify方法通常在每个固定任务集上对所有候选打分，浪费了大量rollout在与目标行为无关的任务上，并且聚合分数会掩盖特定行为的性能回退，导致适应过程既低效又不可靠。

Method: HarnessLens是一个预算感知框架，核心包含三部分：1）联合探索任务空间与用户可配置组件；2）从执行轨迹中推导候选修改方案；3）利用可归因证据门控（attributable-evidence gate）对每个候选仅在与该行为相关的任务上进行选择性验证，避免对无关行为浪费评估预算。

Result: 在三个智能体框架和四个基准测试上，HarnessLens相比竞争基线，平均留出性能提升7.6-13.6%，同时消耗显著更少的评估预算，验证了行为感知验证与显式归因在有限交互预算下能实现更可靠、样本效率更高的框架演化。

Conclusion: 行为感知验证配合显式归因机制可以使智能体框架的自动化演化在受限交互预算下更加可靠和高效；代码已开源。

Abstract: Agent harnesses shape how language-model agents use instructions, tools, and runtime components, but adapting these harnesses requires costly verification. Existing propose-and-verify methods typically score every candidate on a fixed task set, wasting rollouts on unrelated behaviors and allowing aggregate scores to obscure specific regressions. We introduce HarnessLens, a budget-aware framework for automated harness evolution. HarnessLens jointly explores the task space and user-configurable components, derives candidate modifications from execution trajectories, and selectively verifies each candidate on behavior-relevant tasks using an attributable-evidence gate. Across three agent harnesses and four benchmarks, HarnessLens improves average held-out performance by 7.6-13.6% while consuming substantially less evaluation budget than competing baselines. These results demonstrate that behavior-aware verification with explicit attribution enables more reliable and sample-efficient harness evolution under constrained interaction budgets. Our code is available at https://github.com/jhxu5214/HarnessLens.

</details>


### [310] [Not All Eval-Awareness Is Equal: Capabilities Framing Predicts Compliance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27340)
*Allison Zhuang,Santiago Aranguri*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文挑战了安全评估管线中将"评估感知"（eval-awareness）视为单一可抑制量的传统做法，证明其可通过思维链识别为能力导向、安全导向、两者兼具或两者皆非四种框架，且不同框架对模型合规行为的预测截然不同。在Qwen3-32B和FORTRESS数据集上，能力框架比安全框架对合规性的预测高出24-46个百分点，并通过CoT预填充干预验证了因果性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 安全评估管线中常用的"引导干预"（steering interventions）旨在抑制模型的评估感知，但现有方法将评估感知视作单一同质的概念。作者怀疑这种简化掩盖了其内部异质性——评估感知可能包含不同语义框架，而这些框架对行为的影响方向可能相反，导致聚合抑制率的解读失真。

Method: 在Qwen3-32B模型上利用FORTRESS数据集进行分析。首先通过思维链（chain-of-thought）文本将言语化的评估感知分类为四类框架：能力导向、安全导向、两者兼具或两者皆非。随后在不同steering条件下比较各框架对合规性的预测差异。最后，对评估感知为负的rollouts施加CoT预填充干预，检验评估感知与合规行为之间的因果关系。

Result: 能力导向框架与安全导向框架对合规行为的预测差异巨大，前者比后者高出24到46个百分点（在所有测试的steering条件下均成立）。因果验证实验中，11个预填充中有10个使合规行为按预测方向偏移。此外，聚合抑制率可能移动而安全相关成分保持不变化，表明"X%的评估感知抑制"可能对应本质上不同的行为结果。

Conclusion: 评估感知并非行为上均匀的统一量，而是一个由不同语义框架组成的复合构造。安全评估管线不能仅依赖聚合层面的评估感知抑制率，必须区分其成分（尤其是安全导向成分）才能正确解读和设计引导干预。

Abstract: Steering interventions targeting eval-awareness, a model's recognition that it is being tested, are increasingly used in safety evaluation pipelines, where evaluation-awareness is treated as a single quantity to be suppressed. We show that verbalized eval-awareness in chain-of-thought can be identified as capabilities-flavored ("the user is testing my ability to follow instructions"), safety-flavored ("the user is testing my boundaries"), both, or neither: framings that predict compliance very differently. On Qwen3-32B over the FORTRESS dataset, capabilities-framing predicts compliance with a +24 to +46 percentage-point gap over safety-framing across all tested steering conditions. A CoT-prefill intervention on eval-awareness-negative rollouts suggests the link is causal, with 10 of 11 prefills shifting compliance in the predicted direction. Then, eval-awareness is not behaviorally uniform: aggregate suppression rates can move while the safety-relevant component does not, and the same "X% suppression of eval-awareness" can correspond to qualitatively different behavioral outcomes.

</details>


### [311] [Sophistication in GenAI Use: Field Evidence from a Large Firm](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27364)
*Nicholas J. Hallman,Zachary T. Kowaleski,Anu Puvvada,Jaime J. Schmidt*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 基于一家大型企业近4000名后台员工、约71万条提示词及LLM响应的专有数据，研究生成式AI使用的成熟度差异。发现：资深员工使用更成熟；战略性职能（战略、数字创新、项目管理）使用成熟度最高；成熟度随时间无改善，正式AI培训亦无持久提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生成式AI在企业后台办公场景中的广泛采用已是不争事实，但关于员工如何使用AI的成熟度（sophistication）差异及其决定因素，缺乏大规模实证证据。管理者需了解哪些因素提升AI使用成效，研究者也缺乏相关度量基准。

Method: 使用一家大型企业2025年8个月间的专有数据，覆盖15个职能领域近4000名后台员工，收集713,564条员工提示词及对应LLM响应，对AI使用成熟度进行测度与比较分析。

Result: 三个主要发现：（1）资深员工的genAI使用成熟度更高，符合领域专长与genAI能力互补的假设；（2）成熟度在不同职能间差异显著，战略、数字创新和项目管理三类职能最高，三者共同点在于聚焦公司级战略举措与组织变革；（3）成熟度随时间既无改善，正式AI培训后也无持久提升，表明成熟使用的提升具有较高难度。

Conclusion: 该研究为管理者改进genAI使用成效提供了可操作的度量和洞察，为研究者提供了可供未来研究借鉴的成熟度框架与实证基础，同时揭示了通过培训提升使用成熟度所面临的现实挑战。

Abstract: We study how sophistication in generative AI (genAI) use varies among the back-office workforce of a large firm. Using proprietary data, we observe 713,564 employee prompts and their corresponding large language model responses from nearly 4,000 back-office employees across 15 functional areas over eight months in 2025. We document three main findings. First, senior employees exhibit more sophisticated genAI use, consistent with domain expertise complementing genAI capabilities. Second, sophistication varies considerably across functions and is highest in Strategy, Digital Innovation, and Project Management, three groups that share a focus on firmwide strategic initiatives and organizational change. Third, we observe neither improvements in sophistication over time nor lasting improvements following formal AI training, suggesting that sophisticated use can be difficult to change. Together, our study provides measures of and insights into sophisticated genAI use that managers can use to improve outcomes and that researchers can use in future research.

</details>


### [312] [CorporateBench: Large-Scale Q&A Benchmarking with Temporal Knowledge Bases](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27391)
*Sil Hamilton,Albert Yu Sun,Oscar J. Romero,Carl-Leander Henneking,David Mimno,Bishan Yang,Igor Labutov*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出CorporateBench（CB），一个人类验证的多任务问答基准，评估语料库超过23万份文档，规模接近企业通信网络中的真实条件。CB覆盖信息抽取和知识库查询两个维度，通过四个合成企业（12到10,000名员工）进行评测，发现五个LLM在输入规模接近真实场景时性能持续下降。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 企业级大型文档集上的问答能力日益重要，但评估困难：公司不愿共享内部通信，而现有合成数据集过于简单，无法反映真实企业通信网络的复杂性和规模。

Method: 构建CorporateBench基准，从随时间演化的知识库中采样生成四个合成企业语料库（员工规模从12到10,000），保证跨文档逻辑一致性。该基准评估LLM在信息提取和知识库查询两个维度上的表现，并在23万+文档规模下测试五个LLM。

Result: 五个LLM在CorporateBench上的表现随输入规模接近真实规模而显著下降，揭示了现有模型在企业级文档推理方面的不足。

Conclusion: CorporateBench填补了企业通信推理评估领域的空白，为LLM开发者提供了接近真实规模条件下衡量企业通信推理能力的指标。

Abstract: LLMs are increasingly able to answer complex questions about enterprise-scale document collections. But evaluation is hard: companies don't want to share internal communications, and synthetic datasets have been overly simple. We present CorporateBench (CB), a human-validated multi-task Q&A benchmark whose scale approaches the conditions LLMs encounter in corporate communication networks, with evaluation corpora surpassing 230,000 documents. CB evaluates LLMs across two dimensions (information extraction and knowledge base querying) through four synthetically generated firms ranging from 12 to 10,000 employees. Each corpus is sampled from a temporally evolving knowledge base describing a consistent world, guaranteeing cross-document logical consistency even across hundreds of thousands of documents. We evaluate five LLMs on CB, revealing increasingly poor performance as input size approaches realistic scales. CB provides LLM developers a metric for corporate communication reasoning, filling a crucial gap in the benchmarking ecosystem.

</details>


### [313] [Learning a Continuous Sepsis Severity Score Without Hour-by-Hour Supervision: A Two-Site Retrospective Study](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27421)
*Kevin Zhu,Ryan Zhang,Baraa Abed,Tilendra Choudhary,Malvern Madondo,Mehak Arora,Yixuan Yang,Alasdair Gent,Aditya Nagori,Omer T. Inan,Krista L. Haines,Patrick Georgoff,Suresh M. Agarwal,Vijay Krishnamoorthy,Tetsu Ohnuma,Mihai V. Podgoreanu,Michael R. Pinsky,Gilles Clermont,Craig M. Coopersmith,Craig S. Jabaley,Rishikesan Kamaleswaran*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文开发了一种基于患者72小时临床轨迹的败血症严重程度指数（sepsis index），使用43个常规记录的变量，将死亡率作为治疗层面的排序信号而非每状态目标来训练模型。在两个医疗系统共36,807名败血症患者（Sepsis-3标准）上验证，该指数在0-10分范围内能区分存活与非存活患者（高出1.19-1.64分），与乳酸变化相关（rho=0.39），跨机构一致性良好，可能作为辅助临床判断的决策支持工具。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有败血症严重程度指数（如SOFA）依赖几十年前校准的固定变量和权重，离散化粗糙，且不再反映当代重症医学实践。目前没有从患者轨迹直接学习得到的替代指数在常规临床中使用。作者旨在开发一种直接从患者纵向轨迹学习的败血症严重程度指数，以更好地反映现代危重症监护中的预后信息。

Method: 采用回顾性两队列研究设计，涵盖马萨诸塞州和佐治亚州两个医疗系统的29,116例和7,691例满足Sepsis-3标准的成人患者。使用72小时治疗窗口内43个常规记录变量建立败血症指数。关键创新在于使用死亡率作为治疗层面的排序信号（而非每状态目标），允许信用在时间步间非均匀分配。评估使用20%永久保留测试集，通过临床案例和Spearman相关分析，并使用bootstrap重采样获得不确定区间。模型训练在不同站点上进行跨机构验证。

Result: 在0-10分范围内，非存活者在所有基线SOFA-2分层中比存活者高1.19-1.64分，在乳酸、平均动脉压和肌酐分层中结果类似。指数随时间的改变与乳酸改变相关（Spearman rho=0.39，n=1,854），与MAP和肌酐的相关性较弱。跨机构一致性达到同站点相关性的70-77%。外部患者内相关性为0.54和0.59（上限分别为0.92和0.90）。指数与既有指标相关，而零对照接近零。

Conclusion: 该败血症指数展现了逐时的预后信息能力，能够有意义地区分患者结局，并与临床预期一致，表明其作为辅助临床判断的决策支持工具具有潜力。但仍需进一步前瞻性验证以确认其在实际临床环境中的适用性。

Abstract: Currently used sepsis severity indices rely on fixed variables and weights established decades ago, which are coarsely discretized and calibrated to a cohort that no longer reflects contemporary critical care. No alternative learned directly from patient trajectories is in routine use. We conducted a retrospective two-cohort study on a total of 29,116 and 7,691 adult patients meeting Sepsis-3 criteria from two hospital systems in Massachusetts and Georgie, respectively.
  We developed a sepsis index using 43 routinely charted variables over a 72-hour treatment window. Unlike previous studies, we use mortality as a treatment-level ranking signal rather than a per-state target, allowing credit to be redistributed non-uniformly across timesteps. Evaluation was done on a permanent 20% test holdout, using clinical vignettes and Spearman correlation. Uncertainty intervals were obtained by bootstrap resampling of whole patients. Under this ranking scheme, non-survivors scored 1.19-1.64 points higher than survivors on a 0-10 scale within all strata of baseline SOFA-2, with similar results stratifying within lactate, mean arterial pressure (MAP), and creatinine. Within-patient change in the index correlated with change in lactate (Spearman rho = 0.39; n = 1,854). Similar, weaker correlations were found for MAP and creatinine. On a cohort level, cross-institutional agreement measured by Spearman correlation between models trained on different sites, were 70-77% of same-site correlation. External within-patient correlations were 0.54 and 0.59 against ceilings of 0.92 and 0.90. Our index also correlated with established indices, while null controls stayed near zero.
  Our index demonstrated hourly prognostic information that meaningfully separates patient outcomes and is consistent with clinical expectation, indicating potential as a decision support tool complementing clinical judgement.

</details>


### [314] [Mechanistic Reaction Prediction via Discrete Flow Matching on Graph-Structured Electron Occupation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27429)
*Nguyen Xuan-Vu,Octavian Susanu,Daniel Armstrong,Philippe Schwaller*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: MAELLE将化学反应建模为电子占据向量上的离散流匹配（连续时间马尔可夫链），而非传统的分子生成或拓扑图编辑方法，在USPTO-480K基准上达到竞争性能，并在分布外泛化和机理轨迹恢复方面表现优越。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 化学反应本质上是电子空间的变换，但现有机器学习方法要么通过从头生成产物分子，要么通过启发式图编辑直接操作分子拓扑，都未直接建模电子层面的反应机制。

Method: 将反应物到产物的映射建模为图结构整数型电子占据空间上的连续时间马尔可夫链（CTMC）；将离散流匹配混合路径推广到电子重排，利用最优传输构建中间编辑轨迹，无需基元步骤标注即可生成机理可解释的编辑序列。

Result: 在USPTO-480K基准上达到与领先反应预测模型相当的竞争性能；在结构复杂度和反应类型两种分布外设置下保持强性能；能恢复与已知化学一致的机理轨迹并预测副产物。

Conclusion: 在电子空间建模反应比在分子拓扑层面建模更本质且更具鲁棒性，同时自然获得机理可解释性和副产物预测能力。

Abstract: Chemical reactions are fundamentally transformations in electron space, yet most machine learning approaches model them either through \textit{de novo} generation of product molecules or through heuristic graph edits that operate directly on molecular topology.
  We introduce MAELLE (\textbf{M}ech\textbf{A}nistic \textbf{E}dit f\textbf{L}ow-matching on e\textbf{L}ectron r\textbf{E}arrangements), which instead models reactions as discrete flow matching over electron occupation vectors.
  Concretely, we formulate the reactant-to-product mapping as a Continuous-time Markov Chain (CTMC) over the graph-structured integer-valued electron occupation space defined on all bonding, non-bonding, and hydrogen sites.
  To construct the intermediate edit trajectories, we generalize the discrete flow matching mixture path to discrete electron rearrangements using Optimal Transport, yielding a sequence of mechanistically interpretable edit moves without requiring elementary step annotations.
  MAELLE achieves competitive performance on the USPTO-480K benchmark compared with leading reaction prediction models.
  Beyond in-distribution accuracy, we evaluate robustness across two out-of-distribution settings - structural complexity and reaction type - and find that MAELLE maintains strong performance where existing methods degrade.
  Finally, because the learned flow operates over the full electron redistribution, MAELLE naturally recovers mechanistic trajectories that align with known chemistry and can predict side products of a reaction.

</details>


### [315] [WikiSkill: Compiling Agent Experience into Persistent Knowledge for Skill Evolution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27454)
*Liyan Tang,Cyrus Rashtchian,Chun-Sung Ferng,Andrew Tomkins,Da-Cheng Juan,Tu Vu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出WikiSkill框架，通过持续将代理经验整合到持久化知识库（wiki）中，并与代理技能协同演化，使技能演化能够反复利用累积的知识，在多个基准和模型上优于现有技能演化方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的技能自动发现方法虽然能让代理通过交互逐步适应，但指导技能开发的洞察通常分散在优化历史中，缺乏系统性复用，限制了跨迭代的演化效率。

Method: WikiSkill将原始执行经验、累积知识和可执行技能三者分离，持续将经验沉淀为wiki知识库中的知识，后续的技能更新可在此基础上构建，实现技能与知识库的协同演化。

Result: 在多种基准和模型上，WikiSkill一致优于现有技能演化方法；技能演化与模型规模互补，大模型从演化技能中获益更多，小模型配合技能可超越更大的无技能模型；演化技能可跨模型和模型家族迁移，其他模型演化的技能甚至可能优于自演化技能；消融实验证实wiki中持久化的知识累积对技能演化至关重要。

Conclusion: 系统性地累积和精炼代理经验对开发可复用、可迁移的技能至关重要，WikiSkill证明了持续知识库与技能协同演化的有效性。

Abstract: Agent skills package specialized knowledge and workflows into reusable resources that extend AI agent capabilities. Recent work automatically discovers such skills from agent experience, which enables agents to progressively adapt through interaction. However, the insights that guide skill development typically remain scattered across optimization histories, limiting their systematic reuse across iterations. We introduce WikiSkill, a framework that co-evolves agent skills with a persistent knowledge base (wiki). At a high level, WikiSkill separates raw execution experience, accumulated knowledge, and executable skills, while continuously consolidating experience into the wiki, which subsequent skill updates can build on. Across diverse benchmarks and models, WikiSkill consistently outperforms state-of-the-art skill-evolution methods and improves over no-skill baselines in most model-benchmark settings. We find that skill evolution complements model scaling: larger models generally benefit more from evolved skills, while smaller models with skills can outperform substantially larger models without them. We also find that evolved skills transfer effectively across models and model families, and skills evolved by other models can outperform self-evolved skills. Finally, our ablation studies confirm that persistent knowledge accumulation in the wiki is critical for effective skill evolution. These results demonstrate the benefits of systematically accumulating and refining agent experience for developing reusable and transferable skills.

</details>


<div id='cs.CE'></div>

# cs.CE [[Back]](#toc)

### [316] [Report of the 2026 Workshop on Next-Generation Ecosystems for Scientific Computing: Harnessing Community, Software, and AI for Cross-Disciplinary Team Science](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26519)
*Lois Curfman McInnes,Dorian Arnold,Prasanna Balaprakash,Mike Bernhardt,Franck Cappello,Beth Cerny,Deborah DiazGranados,Anshu Dubey,Nichole Etienne,Roscoe Giles,Diego Gomez-Zara,Denice Ward Hood,Mary Ann Leung,Vanessa Lopez-Marrero,Olivia B. Newton,Irene Qualters,Keita Teranishi,Stefan M. Wild,Gabrielle Allen,Richard Arthur,Alexandra Ballow,Tony Baylis,David E. Bernholdt,Daniel Bielich,Johanna Cohoon,Jeremy Crampton,Charles Ferenbaugh,Stephen M. Fiore,Thomas Herault,Tanzima Islam,Stephen Jacobsohn,Meifeng Lin,Charles Lively,Satoshi Matsuoka,Stasa Milojevic,Daniel Nichols,Chris Oehmen,Santiago Ospina Tabares,Michael E. Papka,Katherine Riley,Damian Rouson,Sudip K. Seal,Brittany Segundo,John Shalf,Andrew Siegel,Valerie Taylor,Jim Willenbring,Lou Woodley*

Main category: cs.CE

TL;DR: 本报告综合了2026年下一代科学计算生态系统研讨会的成果，提出了四个相互依存的战略主题（AI赋能的软件生态系统、信任验证与可追溯性、人机协同与范式转变、劳动力与治理），并将其转化为覆盖共享研究基础设施、信任与可追溯性、用户体验、人机协同、劳动力发展、跨部门协调、管理与可持续性以及科学价值评估等八个社区行动优先事项。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 科学计算领域正因人工智能、异构计算、自动化和数据密集型研究而发生深刻变革，这些变化不仅影响计算工具本身，还重塑了科研机构、劳动力模型和协作实践。需要系统地规划和引导科学计算生态系统的未来发展。

Method: 通过举办2026年下一代科学计算生态系统研讨会（三年系列中的第二年），采用社会-技术协同设计的视角，邀请领域专家进行讨论，从讨论中提炼四个战略主题，并进一步转化为八个可执行的社区优先行动事项。

Result: 确定四项战略主题并产出八项行动优先事项，涵盖软件生态、可信度、人机协同、劳动力、跨部门协作、可持续性及科学价值评估等关键维度。

Conclusion: 报告指出，构建可信赖、可持续、创新且具有韧性的科学计算生态系统需要在AI逐步主导科研工作的背景下持续推进社区协作和社会-技术协同设计。

Abstract: Scientific computing is undergoing rapid transformation as advances in artificial intelligence, heterogeneous computing, automation, and data-intensive research reshape not only computational tools but also the institutions, workforce models, and collaborative practices that support scientific discovery. This report synthesizes insights from the 2026 Workshop on Next-Generation Ecosystems for Scientific Computing, the second in a three-year series focused on strengthening scientific computing ecosystems through socio-technical co-design. Workshop discussions identified four interdependent strategic themes: software ecosystems for AI-enabled scientific discovery; trust, validation, and traceability; human-AI teaming and paradigm shifts; and workforce, pedagogy, and governance. The report translates these themes into eight priorities for community action spanning shared research infrastructure, trust and traceability, user experience, human-AI teaming, workforce development, cross-sector coordination, stewardship and sustainability, and evaluation of scientific value. Together, these priorities outline directions for building scientific computing ecosystems that remain trustworthy, sustainable, innovative, and resilient as AI assumes a growing role in scientific work.

</details>


<div id='cs.GT'></div>

# cs.GT [[Back]](#toc)

### [317] [Simultaneous Envy and Equitability Guarantees](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26410)
*Hadi Hosseini,Shraddha Pathak,Lirong Xia,Chengkai Zhang*

Main category: cs.GT

TL;DR: 本文研究公平分配中两种根本不同的公平概念——无嫉妒性（envy-freeness）与公平性（equitability）——的兼容性，探讨其在不可分物品与家务场景中松弛版本的共存问题与计算复杂度，并首次提出跨概念的ex-ante–ex-post保证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有公平分配研究要么关注同时满足密切相关（closely related）的公平概念，要么在ex-ante和ex-post两个世界分别实现单一概念，缺乏对本质上不同公平概念之间兼容性的系统研究。作者旨在填补这一空白。

Method: 论文对不可分物品（goods）与家务（chores）两类设置分别研究EF1+EQ1及更强的EFX+EQX的存在性与计算复杂度。主要算法结果针对标准化二值物品（binary goods）给出最多七个代理人的EF1+EQ1分配算法，并证明二进制家务在任意代理人数量下（甚至无需标准化）即满足更强的EFX+EQX保证。此外，作者提出跨概念的随机化分配框架，要求ex-ante为一种概念、ex-post为另一种概念。

Result: 1) EF1+EQ1在标准化加法估值下可能不存在；2) 标准化二进制物品在最多七名代理人时存在多项式时间算法计算EF1+EQ1分配；3) 二进制家务对任意代理人数量均满足EFX+EQX，且无需标准化假设；4) 揭示了物品与家务两种设置之间在同时满足不同公平概念方面存在显著差异。

Conclusion: 物品与家务设置在同时兼容无嫉妒性与公平性松弛版本时表现出强烈对比：家务场景显著更易实现（更强保证、更多代理人、无需标准化），而物品场景则受限且复杂。论文开辟了跨概念ex-ante–ex-post保证这一新的研究方向。

Abstract: Recent work in fair division has focused on either simultaneously satisfying closely related fairness notions or achieving a single notion across the ex-ante and ex-post worlds. We study the compatibility of two fundamentally different fairness notions: envy-freeness and equitability. For indivisible goods-only and chores-only settings, we study the existence and complexity of simultaneously satisfying their relaxations, revealing sharp contrasts between the two settings. We show that EF1+EQ1 may fail to exist even for normalized, additive valuations. Our main algorithmic result computes an EF1+EQ1 allocation for normalized binary goods with at most seven agents. In sharp contrast, binary chores admit the stronger EFX+EQX guarantee for any number of agents, even without normalization. We further initiate the study of cross-notion ex-ante--ex-post guarantees, asking whether randomized allocations can provide ex-ante guarantees for one notion while preserving ex-post guarantees for another.

</details>


### [318] [Trusting AI in Competitive Markets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26539)
*Jussi Keppo,Yuze Li,Gerry Tsoukalas,Nuo Yuan*

Main category: cs.GT

TL;DR: 本研究通过实验室实验探讨AI定价建议在寡头定价中如何影响卖家信任与定价行为，发现市场性别构成显著调节AI建议的效果：仅在女性市场中AI建议使价格提高29%、利润提高39%，而在男性及混合市场中无显著效应；一种非齐次隐马尔可夫模型揭示了性别差异化的动态信任学习模式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人们对AI建议的信任在使用过程中出现分化——有人加深信任、有人逐渐丧失信任。现有研究缺乏对这一动态分歧在"建议无法自我证明"环境中的考察，尤其在寡头定价场景下，建议的效果取决于竞争对手反应。理解什么因素导致信任极化对AI治理与平台监管具有重要价值。

Method: 采用实验室实验设计：273名卖家分布在91个三卖家市场中竞争30轮，操纵两个变量——是否存在AI定价建议，以及市场性别构成（女性-only、男性-only或混合）。分析主要采用非齐次隐马尔可夫模型（Non-Homogeneous Hidden Markov Model），以捕捉依赖市场性别构成的动态关联，即盈利轮次如何预测后续对AI建议的遵从变化。

Result: 市场性别构成塑造了卖家从AI建议中学习的模式及最终价格水平。在女性-only市场中，AI建议使价格提高29%、利润提高39%；在男性-only及混合市场中无显著效应。非齐次隐马尔可夫模型揭示：盈利轮次在女性市场中预测对AI遵从度上升，在其他市场中则预测遵从度下降，与"习得性信任"和自我服务归因相一致。这一结果与现有关于性别与AI的文献预测方向相反。

Conclusion: AI建议的实际效果不仅取决于算法本身，还取决于人类使用方的特征及其学习动态；平台治理与监管不应仅聚焦算法，还应关注塑造其影响的人类侧因素。

Abstract: Problem definition: People's trust in AI advice diverges as they use it, deepening for some and eroding for others. We study this divergence in oligopoly pricing, where advice cannot prove itself: rivals' responses decide whether it pays off. Methodology/results: In a laboratory experiment, 273 sellers compete across 91 three-seller markets over 30 rounds; we vary the presence of AI pricing recommendations and the gender composition of the market (female-only, male-only, or mixed). We find that the gender composition of the market shapes how sellers learn from the advice, and where prices settle as a result. In female-only markets, recommendations raise prices by 29% and profits by 39%; in male-only and mixed-gender markets, they have no significant effect. A Non-Homogeneous Hidden Markov Model reveals a composition-specific dynamic association: profitable rounds predict rising adherence to the AI in female-only markets and declining adherence otherwise, a pattern consistent with learned trust and self-serving attribution. The pattern reverses what recent evidence on gender and AI would predict. Managerial implications: We discuss implications for platform governance and regulatory oversight, which should focus not only on the algorithm but on the human side that shapes its effects.

</details>


### [319] [A lone divider allocation algorithm with subjective divisibility](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26801)
*Uriel Feige*

Main category: cs.GT

TL;DR: 本文研究主观可分割分配模型中的最大最小份额（MMS）分配问题，将此前已知的5/9-MMS分配存在性改进为3/5-MMS分配一定存在，证明基于对独裁分割（lone divider）框架的改编。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在主观可分割分配模型中，商品既可以是完全可分的，也可能存在"不可分割块"（小于1的份额价值为0）。此前研究已证明5/9-MMS分配的存在性，但这一界仍有改进空间，需要更强的结果。

Method: 作者对lone divider框架进行改编以适应主观可分割分配模型的结构特点，通过精心设计的分配策略证明3/5-MMS分配的存在性。

Result: 证明了3/5-MMS分配在主观可分割分配模型中一定存在，优于此前已知的5/9-MMS界（5/9≈0.556，3/5=0.6）。

Conclusion: 通过改编lone divider框架，将主观可分割分配模型中的MMS近似比从5/9提升至3/5，为该问题的近似界限提供了新的上界结果。

Abstract: In the subjective divisibility allocation model, all goods are divisible, every agent $i$ has a non-negative additive valuation function $v_i$, where for some goods $v_i$ is additive over fractions of the good, and for some goods, any fraction smaller than~1 has value~0. Previous work established the existence of $\frac{5}{9}$-MMS allocations in this setting. We show that $\frac{3}{5}$-MMS allocations exist. Our proof is based on an adaptation of the lone divider framework.

</details>


### [320] [Robust Lottery Compression for Metric Voting: A Transfer Principle for Bounded Randomness](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26854)
*Jianhao Jia,Bo Peng*

Main category: cs.GT

TL;DR: 本文研究有界随机化社会选择中的度量扭曲问题。核心贡献是：证明了在必须先从K个候选中均匀随机选择的有界随机化模型中，可以使扭曲度逼近当前最优无限制上界5/2。对每个整数K≥802，存在一个扭曲度至多5/2+O(K^{-1/3})的有界随机化规则。论文的技术核心是维度无关压缩定理：如果彩票的扭曲度至多为ρ且支撑集中每个候选人的确定性扭曲度至多为H，则该彩票可以被均匀K项近似，且扭曲度损失仅为O(K^{-1/2})。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 此前研究表明，有界随机化模型（确定性地选出K个候选人的多重集，再均匀随机选择）可以打败最优确定性扭曲度3，但尚未达到无限制随机化模型的最优扭曲度5/2。作者希望证明该受限模型也能逼近这一无限制上界。

Method: 作者提出了一个"维度无关压缩定理"：如果一个彩票的扭曲度至多ρ且其支撑中每个候选人的确定性扭曲度至多H，则可以构造一个均匀K项近似，其扭曲度至多ρ+(H+1)√(π/(8K))。但Mixed Integrated Veto规则不满足支撑条件，因此先剔除早期淘汰的结果，用O(τ²)的扭曲度损失换取每个支撑候选人的确定性扭曲度O(1/τ)上界。平衡修复代价与压缩损失，最终得到O(K^{-1/3})的收敛率。

Result: 对每个整数K≥802，存在有界随机化规则，其扭曲度至多5/2+3(π/(8K))^{1/3}+2√(π/(8K))。因此O(ε^{-3})个条目足以达到扭曲度5/2+ε（与选民和候选人数无关）。此外，164个条目即可实现扭曲度严格低于3，给出最小列表大小N*满足2≤N*≤164。

Conclusion: 有界随机化模型能够逼近无限制随机化的最优扭曲度基准5/2，且只需要O(ε^{-3})个条目即可实现5/2+ε的扭曲度。这缩小了"打破确定性扭曲度3"所需的最小列表大小上界至164。压缩定理提供了通用工具，但Mixed Integrated Veto需要先修复支撑条件，导致收敛率从O(K^{-1/2})降至O(K^{-1/3})。

Abstract: We study metric distortion in randomized social choice under bounded randomness: on every preference profile, the voting rule must deterministically identify a multiset of $K$ candidates and then select a uniformly random entry. Previous work showed that this restricted model can beat the optimal deterministic distortion of $3$. We show that it can in fact approach the current best unrestricted upper benchmark of $5/2$. For every integer $K\ge 802$, there exists a bounded-randomness rule with distortion at most $\frac{5}{2} +3\left(\fracπ{8K}\right)^{1/3} +2\sqrt{\fracπ{8K}}$.
  Consequently, $O(\varepsilon^{-3})$ entries suffice for distortion $5/2+\varepsilon$, independently of the numbers of voters and candidates. We also show that $164$ entries already achieve distortion strictly below $3$, giving $2\le N^\star\le 164$ for the minimum list size needed to break the deterministic barrier. Our main technical contribution is a dimension-free compression theorem: if a lottery has distortion at most $ρ$ and every candidate in its support has deterministic distortion at most $H$, then it admits a uniform $K$-entry approximation with distortion at most $ρ+(H+1)\sqrt{π/(8K)}$. Thus, lotteries whose possible outcomes are already well behaved incur only $O(K^{-1/2})$ compression loss. Mixed Integrated Veto does not satisfy this support condition, so we first remove early-eliminated outcomes, trading $O(τ^2)$ distortion loss for an $O(1/τ)$ bound on the deterministic distortion of every supported candidate. Balancing this repair cost against compression yields the $O(K^{-1/3})$ convergence rate.

</details>


### [321] [Blindfolded pursuit with delays of your choice](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27347)
*Torben Schürenberg,Maximilian J. Stahlberg*

Main category: cs.GT

TL;DR: 本文研究图上单追捕者的追逃博弈，追捕者可为边赋整数旅行时间并指定查询顶点序列；在隐形逃逸者场景下，证明当追捕者有权选择旅行时间时，可在多项式时间内成功追捕任何图上的逃逸者；若逃逸者还可提前未知时间出发，追捕者仍需指数时间才能获胜。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的猎兔博弈和灭火博弈在无权图上需要多个猎手或消防员才能成功，追捕者数量可能随顶点数线性增长。本文探索是否可以通过赋予追捕者分配旅行时间的权力，使得单一追捕者就能在任意图上成功，从而突破多代理人的限制。

Method: 本文将问题形式化为图上单追捕者与隐形逃逸者之间的博弈：追捕者可以为每条边分配整数旅行时间，并指定一个有限长度的查询顶点序列（每时间步查询一个顶点）。研究两种逃逸变体——单位旅行时间下逃逸者每步必须移动的猎兔版本，以及逃逸者可以在顶点等待的灭火版本。通过将有权重设置的追捕策略与无权多代理人场景进行对比，证明权重赋予使单追捕者具有多项式时间获胜策略；对于逃逸者可提前未知时间出发的更一般情形，分析其指数时间上界。

Result: 对于猎兔博弈和灭火博弈两种设定，当追捕者有权为边分配旅行时间时，单一追捕者可以在任何图上于多项式时间内成功。这与无权图形成鲜明对比——无权图上所需猎手或消防员数量可随顶点数线性增长。若逃逸者除等待外还可提前在未知时间出发，追捕者仍可在任何图上获胜，但需要指数时间。

Conclusion: 旅行时间权重的分配能力本质性地改变了博弈的复杂性，使单一追捕者能够克服多代理人场景下的数量劣势；但在逃逸者拥有更灵活的先手优势时（提前出发），时间代价显著上升至指数级。

Abstract: We study pursuit-evasion games on graphs with a single pursuer and an invisible evader. The pursuer may assign integer travel times to the edges of the graph and specify a finite sequence of vertices to query, one per time step. The evader then chooses a walk over the same time horizon, aiming to elude all queries. With unit travel times, the setting in which the evader must move at every time step is known as the hunter and rabbit game, while the variant in which the evader can wait at a vertex can be phrased as a firefighting game: the vertices of a burning graph must be extinguished, and any vertex left burning reignites its neighbors. For both settings, we show that the power to choose travel times allows a single pursuer to succeed in polynomial time on any graph. This contrasts with unweighted graphs, where the number of hunters or firefighters needed can grow linearly in the number of vertices. If the evader, in addition to waiting, may start at an earlier time unknown to the pursuer, we show that the pursuer still wins on any graph given exponential time.

</details>


### [322] [Token-Level Advertising](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27382)
*Hanbing Liu,Bowei Zhang,Changyuan Yu,Yinyu Ye,Qi Qi*

Main category: cs.GT

TL;DR: 本文提出LAMA（隐式广告主混合拍卖），一种面向生成式AI的token级广告机制，将广告主影响力直接嵌入生成过程，满足马尔可夫DSIC和IR，实现近最优KL正则化福利，并在真实商业搜索查询上提升平台福利与收入且保持用户体验质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生成式AI正在改变人们获取信息的方式，传统基于预定义广告位的广告机制不再适用。需要一种"生成原生"的广告机制，将广告主的出价和影响直接嵌入到token生成过程中，而非依赖预定义的广告插槽。

Method: LAMA机制包括：(1) 广告主报告局部续价值（local continuation values），诱导出广告主特定的下一token策略；(2) 平台通过隐式混合解码，同时更新分配后验；(3) 满足马尔可夫DSIC与个体理性（IR）；(4) 实现近最优的KL正则化福利；(5) 提出基于学习的实现方案，从学习的局部优势和根值在线重构所需报告。

Result: 在真实商业搜索查询拆分上的概念验证实验表明，LAMA在提升平台福利和收入的同时，保持面向用户的响应质量，为生成原生广告的可行性提供了初步证据。

Conclusion: LAMA证明了将拍卖机制与生成过程深度融合的可行性，在激励机制兼容性、社会福利最优性和实际收益之间取得了平衡，为生成式AI时代的原生广告提供了新范式。

Abstract: Generative AI is transforming how people access information, challenging traditional advertising mechanisms built around predefined slots. Towards generation-native advertising, we propose the Latent Advertiser Mixture Auction (LAMA), a token-level advertising mechanism that embeds advertiser influence directly into the generation process. Advertisers report local continuation values that induce advertiser-specific next-token policies, from which the platform decodes through a latent mixture while updating an allocation posterior. We show that LAMA satisfies Markov DSIC and IR, and achieves near-optimal KL-regularized welfare. We further develop a learning-based implementation that reconstructs the required reports online from learned local advantages and root values. Proof-of-concept experiments on real-world commercial-search query splits show that LAMA improves platform welfare and revenue while maintaining user-facing response quality, providing initial evidence for the feasibility of generation-native advertising.

</details>


### [323] [Algorithms for Robbins' Problem using Markov Decision Processes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27419)
*Léonard Brice,F. Thomas Bruss,Anirban Majumdar,Jean-François Raskin*

Main category: cs.GT

TL;DR: 本文研究Robbins问题（秘书选择问题的全信息变体），通过将其建模为无限马尔可夫决策过程（MDP），提出多种有限状态抽象方法以近似求解最优期望排名。作者给出了n≤100范围内所有实例的近似值，其中n=5到100的近似结果优于此前已知的任何结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Robbins问题是秘书问题中具有挑战性的变体，其最优期望排名最小化需要记住过去所有候选人的值（全信息），这使得分析十分困难。此前仅知道n≤4的精确值，数值近似也仅限于个位数（n不超过一位数）的实例。作者希望为更大的n提供更好的近似，填补这一空白。

Method: 首先将Robbins问题实例建模为无限马尔可夫决策过程（MDP），然后提出多种有限状态抽象来近似这些MDP的价值函数。尽管全记忆必要，作者发现了简单记忆结构足以获得接近最优的选择策略。通过这些抽象方法，对每个n≤100计算出近似值。

Result: 作者为n≤100的Robbins问题提供了近似值。对于所有5≤n≤100的实例，给出的近似结果均优于此前已知的任何近似值（此前仅n≤4有精确值，数值近似仅针对个位数n）。

Conclusion: 本文展示了有限状态抽象方法可以有效逼近Robbins问题的价值函数，且简单的记忆结构即可获得接近最优的策略，为更大规模的秘书问题变体提供了实用的计算求解框架。

Abstract: In this paper, we consider Robbins' problem, which is a full information variant of the well-known secretary selection problem. In this version of the problem, the goal is to minimize the expected rank of the selected candidate among $n$ that are interviewed sequentially, and a decision to select or not the $m^{th}$ candidate needs to be taken right after the interview (so without seeing the last $n-m$ candidates and without recall). We first show how to model instances of Robbins' problem as infinite Markov Decision Processes (MDPs). Then we propose several finite-state abstractions of these MDPs that allow us to approximate the value of the problem for fixed $n$. While it is known that the full memory of past candidates' values is necessary for optimal expected rank minimization, making the analysis of the problem challenging, we highlight simple memory structures that are sufficient for obtaining near-optimal selection strategies. Additionally, we provide approximate values for Robbins' problem for numbers of candidates $n$ up to 100 for which no good approximations were previously known (the exact value is only known for instances where $n \leq 4$ and numerical approximations were for small values of $n$ not exceeding one digit), for all $n : 5 \leq n \leq 100$, we give better approximation than what was previously known.

</details>


<div id='cs.GR'></div>

# cs.GR [[Back]](#toc)

### [324] [Comparative Evaluation of 3D Reconstruction Methods for Immersive Visualization of Laboratory Objects](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27301)
*Brian De La Cruz,Aaron Y. Zhao,Maitrey Gramopadhye,Sawyer J. Lazar,Xianming Tan,Daniel Szafir,David S. Lawrence*

Main category: cs.GR

TL;DR: 本研究比较了四种3D重建方法（摄影测量、NeRF、高斯溅射、LiDAR）用于生成实验室物品的全息教学模型，发现NeRF方法在透明、反射或低纹理物体上表现最佳，形状和颜色重建优于纹理，并为AR/MR教育环境中的沉浸式学习对象提供了实用工作流。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索当前3D重建方法是否足以支持创建逼真的实验室物品全息表征用于教育用途，填补教育全息重建方法系统比较的研究空白。

Method: 比较四种3D重建方法（摄影测量、NeRF、高斯溅射、LiDAR），用它们生成常见实验室物品的全息模型，由研究生采用重复测量设计从形状、颜色、纹理和视觉缺陷四个维度评估模型保真度。

Result: NeRF方法在各类物体上产生了最一致的高保真表征，尤其对透明、反射或低纹理物品表现突出；形状和颜色的重建成功率普遍高于纹理，说明某些视觉属性在全息教育模型中仍较难准确再现。

Conclusion: 研究不仅明确了各重建方法的优劣，还展示了一种创建沉浸式学习对象的实用工作流，可用于实验室前准备、空间推理和AR/MR教育环境中的学生参与，为教育者和研究者的沉浸式数字学习体验开发提供了设计相关见解。

Abstract: In this study, we examined whether current 3D reconstruction methods can support the creation of realistic holographic representations of laboratory objects for educational use. In this regard, we compared four approaches: photogrammetry, a neural radiance field (NeRF)-based method, Gaussian splatting, and LiDAR. These methods were used to generate holographic models of common laboratory items and their fidelity was evaluated by graduate students. Participants assessed the models for shape, color, texture, and visual defects using a repeated-measures design. Across objects, the NeRF-based method produced the most consistently high-fidelity representations, particularly for transparent, reflective, or low-texture items that were difficult to capture with other approaches. Shape and color were generally reproduced more successfully than texture, suggesting that some visual properties remain more challenging to represent accurately in educational holograms. Beyond identifying the strengths and limitations of each reconstruction method, the study demonstrates a practical workflow for creating immersive learning objects that may support pre-laboratory preparation, spatial reasoning, and student engagement in AR/MR-based educational environments. These findings offer design-relevant insights for educators and researchers developing immersive digital learning experiences.

</details>


<div id='cs.LG'></div>

# cs.LG [[Back]](#toc)

### [325] [GRAS: Guided Reduced-Variance Proposals and Adaptive Selection for Training-Free Reward Alignment in Discrete Diffusion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26585)
*Kwanyoung Kim*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出GRAS方法，通过低方差梯度估计（Rao-Blackwell化揭示和leave-one-out基线）与自适应重采样温度（group-relative advantage），改进离散扩散模型的训练免费推理时引导，在DNA调控和蛋白质设计任务上超越现有训练免费方法，达到或超越奖励微调模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 离散扩散模型在序列生成中广泛应用，推理时无需重训练即可引导其朝下游奖励方向生成变得越来越重要。现有的训练免费引导通过梯度引导和搜索两种方式结合进行，但该结合存在两个弱点：引导提议从单个含噪样本估计梯度导致方差过大；搜索以固定温度重采样粒子，忽略了奖励在每个去噪步骤的分布差异。

Method: 提出GRAS方法，包含两方面改进：（1）针对提议分布，对可微奖励使用Rao-Blackwell化揭示降低估计器方差，对不可微奖励使用leave-one-out基线；（2）针对搜索重采样，将每步奖励值标准化为组内相对优势（group-relative advantage），并证明其可归结为一个核心成分：自适应重采样温度。这些改进不增加任何去噪器计算成本。

Result: 在调控性DNA和蛋白质设计任务上，GRAS取得了最佳的训练免费奖励效果，超越先前的训练免费方法，且达到或超越奖励微调模型的性能，即使对于不可微奖励也能保持有效性。

Conclusion: GRAS简单而有效，通过同时解决梯度估计方差和搜索温度两个问题，实现了更优的训练免费离散扩散模型引导，且无需额外的去噪器计算开销。

Abstract: Discrete diffusion models have become a strong, widely adopted class of generators for sequence data, and steering them toward a downstream reward at inference time, without any retraining, is increasingly important. Such training-free steering is done by gradient guidance, by search, or by combining the two. We study the combined regime and identify two weaknesses in how it is usually run: the guided proposal estimates its gradient from a single noisy sample, and the search then resamples particles at a fixed temperature that ignores how rewards spread across each denoising step. We address both with a small set of changes that add no denoiser cost. For the proposal, we lower the estimator variance with a Rao-Blackwellized reveal for differentiable rewards and a leave-one-out baseline for non-differentiable ones; for the search, we standardize the per-step values into a group-relative advantage and prove it collapses to a single active ingredient, an adaptive resampling temperature. We call the resulting method Guided Reduced-variance proposals and Adaptive Selection (GRAS). GRAS is simple yet effective: across regulatory DNA and protein design it attains the best training-free reward, outperforming prior training-free methods and matching or surpassing a reward-fine-tuned model, and it remains effective even for non-differentiable rewards.

</details>


### [326] [SLM-Conditioned Hierarchical Relation Routing for Labeled Property Graph Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26132)
*Michal Podstawski*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出SLM条件层级关系路由架构，将小型语言模型直接集成到图消息选择中，用于带标签的属性图学习。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统图神经网络将节点和关系的异构属性和文本表示为静态特征向量，无法动态判断哪些语义证据应影响特定预测目标的消息传播。

Method: 拓扑GNN提供稳定结构表示和预测锚点；每个目标节点的入射消息结合邻居结构状态、节点属性编码、关系属性编码和关系类型；参数高效的小型语言模型处理结构化图软令牌并生成目标条件路由查询，先在关系类型内选择相关消息，再跨关系级摘要路由信息，最终以有界残差更新更新拓扑锚点。

Result: 该架构支持邻居级和关系类型级的可解释分析，在保留结构证据的同时允许上下文语义信息修改预测，提供将语言派生语义整合到属性丰富的图学习中的通用机制。

Conclusion: 该工作为属性图中语言语义与结构信息的深度集成提供了新思路，兼顾可解释性与预测灵活性。

Abstract: Labeled property graphs combine relational structure with heterogeneous textual and categorical properties attached to both nodes and relationships. Conventional graph neural networks typically represent these properties as static feature vectors, limiting their ability to determine which semantic evidence should influence message propagation for a particular prediction target. We propose SLM-Conditioned Hierarchical Relation Routing, an architecture that integrates a small language model directly into graph message selection. A topology GNN provides a stable structural representation and prediction anchor. For each target node, incident messages combine the neighbor's structural state, node-property encoding, relationship-property encoding, and relationship type. A parameter-efficient SLM processes structured graph soft tokens and produces a target-conditioned routing query. This query first selects relevant messages within each relationship type and subsequently routes information across relation-level summaries. The resulting representation provides a bounded residual update to the topology anchor, preserving structural evidence while allowing contextual semantic information to modify the prediction. The architecture supports interpretable analysis at both the neighbor and relationship-type levels and provides a general mechanism for integrating language-derived semantics into property-rich graph learning.

</details>


### [327] [High Probability Derivative Bounds for Random tanh Neural Networks on a Hypercube](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26526)
*Josef Dick,Michael Feischl,Fabian Zehetgruber*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文针对激活函数导数满足阶乘增长界的宽随机神经网络，建立了混合输入导数的高概率界。特别地，对Xavier初始化的tanh网络，证明在隐藏宽度满足一定条件下，高阶混合导数至多以L^{|u|-1}多项式速度增长，一阶导数界与深度无关，并由此得到Lipschitz常数和加权Sobolev范数的高概率界，连接了QMC积分与训练分析。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 宽随机神经网络是深度学习中广泛使用的基础模型，但其输入导数（特别是高阶混合导数）的行为尚不清楚。现有基于权重矩阵Euclidean算符范数的确定性分析通常给出随深度指数增长的导数界，过于保守。作者旨在通过高斯网络的随机结构获得更紧的高概率导数界，以更好地理解网络的Lipschitz性质、Sobolev正则性及其对QMC积分和训练分析的影响。

Method: 作者将导数中最高阶导数对应的线性项单独分离出来，利用可测有限网（measurable finite nets）控制相应的切方向，从而显著改善指数增长估计。具体分析基于：1) 激活函数导数满足阶乘增长条件；2) 高斯权重和Xavier初始化的分布特性；3) 宽度n满足关于深度L、输入维数n_0及失败概率η的下界条件（n ≥ C(L^3n_0^2(1+log n_0)+L^2(1+log(L/η)))）。

Result: 对标量输出的tanh网络，证明存在常数C,C_0,C_1>0，使得当宽度满足给定下界时，以至少1-η的概率，对所有非空指标集u和所有x∈[0,1]^{n_0}，有|D^u R_{Φ^{(L)}}(x)| ≤ C_0|u|!(C_1L)^{|u|-1}∏_{j∈u}β_j(η,n_0)同时成立。一阶导数界不依赖深度；无平方项混合导数的增长关于阶数|u|至多为L^{|u|-1}多项式。由此导出Euclidean Lipschitz常数和加权Sobolev范数的高概率界。

Conclusion: 该工作表明宽随机tanh网络的高阶导数具有良好的正则性——不是指数增长而是多项式增长，这为理解深度网络的函数空间性质提供了理论基础。所得Sobolev范数界可直接用于QMC积分误差分析，有望为基于QMC的训练方法的收敛性分析提供新的工具。

Abstract: We establish high-probability bounds for mixed input derivatives of wide random neural networks whose activation derivatives satisfy a factorial growth bound. Our main result specializes these estimates to $\tanh$ networks with Xavier initialization. A direct deterministic analysis based on Euclidean operator norms of the weight matrices yields derivative bounds that generally grow exponentially with the depth. We show that this growth can be substantially improved for sufficiently wide Gaussian networks by isolating the term that is linear in the highest-order derivative and controlling the corresponding tangent directions by measurable finite nets.
  For scalar-output $\tanh$ networks with Gaussian weights and Xavier initialization, we prove that there exist constants $C,C_0,C_1>0$ such that, whenever the common hidden width satisfies $n \geq C\left(L^3n_0^2(1+\log n_0)+L^2\left(1+\log(L/η)\right)\right)$, then, with probability at least $1-η$, the estimate $\left|D^u\mathcal{R}_{Φ^{(L)}}(x)\right| \leq C_0 |u|! (C_1L)^{|u|-1}\prod_{j\in u}β_j(η,n_0)$ holds simultaneously for every non-empty $u\subseteq[n_0]$ and every $x\in[0,1]^{n_0}$. Thus, the first-order derivative bound is independent of the depth, while a square-free mixed derivative of order $|u|$ grows at most polynomially as $L^{|u|-1}$, apart from the coordinate factors. As consequences, we obtain high-probability bounds for the Euclidean Lipschitz constant and for weighted Sobolev norms of the network realization. The latter connect the derivative estimates to quasi-Monte Carlo integration and indicate how such regularity can enter the analysis of QMC-based training.

</details>


### [328] [NeuronFuzz: Safety Neuron Guided Fuzzing for LLM Safety Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26222)
*Zhiyuan Xu,Muhammad Firhard Roslan,Joseph Gardiner,Sana Belguith,Lichao Wu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: NeuronFuzz是一个白盒模糊测试框架，利用LLM内部安全神经元的激活值作为连续执行反馈，在prefill阶段即可获得类梯度信号（无需生成响应），从而高效地发现越狱攻击模板。在21个文本和多模态模型上验证，白盒场景下越狱发现率达76-100%，黑盒迁移场景下平均ASR达69.6%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自动化的LLM安全测试方法主要依赖响应级反馈：每个候选提示都需要生成目标模型的完整响应来评估攻击效果。这不仅昂贵，而且在强对齐模型上只能提供稀疏的指导——大多数候选都被以相同的方式拒绝，缺乏可用的区分度信号来引导fuzzing过程的优化。

Method: NeuronFuzz包含两个核心组件：(1) SafetyOracle——利用模板不变的有害/良性输入和稳定性感知选择，识别一组紧凑的安全神经元，将其激活值转换为连续的"安全警报分数"，该分数可在prefill阶段获得（无需响应生成），且是可微的；(2) 基于梯度的模板变异——利用警报分数的梯度定位对安全敏感的位置，结合掩码语言模型生成流畅且上下文兼容的突变，同时保留原始有害载荷并避免引入额外的优化变量。

Result: 在5个白盒源模型上，越狱发现率达到76-100%，比基线高出最多48个百分点。优化后的模板零样本迁移到开源权重模型和6个专有目标模型，平均ASR和top-5集成ASR（EASR）分别达到69.6%/92.6%和44.1%/60.0%。

Conclusion: NeuronFuzz通过将安全神经元的内部激活转化为连续的fuzzing反馈信号，绕过了传统响应级评估的瓶颈，在效率和发现率上均显著优于现有方法，同时展示了良好的跨模型迁移能力。

Abstract: Safety evaluation is critical for assessing whether aligned Large Language Models (LLMs) remain robust against jailbreak attacks. Existing automated testing methods, however, largely rely on response-level feedback: each candidate prompt typically requires generating a target-model response to evaluate its attack effectiveness. This process is expensive and, more importantly, provides only sparse guidance on strongly aligned models, where most candidates are rejected with the same failure outcome.
  This paper presents NeuronFuzz, a white-box fuzzing framework that exploits internal safety neurons as continuous execution feedback for LLM safety evaluation. A SafetyOracle converts safety-neuron activations into a continuous safety alarm score that serves as feedback for fuzzing and can be obtained during prefill, eliminating response generation from the fuzzing loop. To construct the SafetyOracle, NeuronFuzz uses template-invariant harmful and benign inputs and stability-aware selection to identify a compact set of safety neurons whose activations capture harmful-intent recognition. Moreover, since the safety alarm score is differentiable, NeuronFuzz uses its gradients to identify safety-sensitive template positions and a masked language model to generate fluent, context-compatible mutations while preserving original harmful payload and avoiding additional optimization variables. We evaluate NeuronFuzz across 21 text and multimodal models. Across five white-box source models, it achieves a 76-100% jailbreak discovery rate, outperforming baselines by up to 48 percentage points. Its optimized templates further transfer zero-shot to open-weight and six proprietary target models, achieving average ASR and top-5 ensemble ASR (EASR) of 69.6%/92.6% and 44.1%/60.0%, respectively.

</details>


### [329] [Pruning Binarized Neural Networks: A Dedicated Framework and Globally Weighted Algorithms](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26233)
*Roan Rubiales,Jean Pierre David*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文介绍了一个基于PyTorch的研究导向框架，用于二值化神经网络的冻结与剪枝设计与优化，并提出了一种跨抽象层级的全局权重剪枝方法，在VGG11上实现70%的剪枝率且精度保持不变，远超现有二值化设定下的41%最佳结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度神经网络极端压缩（如完全二值化）能大幅降低内存占用和算术复杂度，便于在FPGA和微控制器等受限边缘硬件上部署。尽管结合二值化与剪枝有望带来额外效率提升，但现有剪枝策略不适用于二值化表示，且难以转化为实际的硬件收益。

Method: 提出了一个基于PyTorch的研究导向框架，集成了冻结（freezing）和剪枝（pruning）机制。在该框架基础上，提出一种新的剪枝方法，通过考虑跨抽象层级学习参数的相对重要性，设计全局加权机制来指导剪枝决策。

Result: 该方法在模型精度与剪枝率之间取得了更优的权衡，在VGG11上实现了70%的剪枝率且精度保持不变，而现有最优方法在二值化设定下仅能达到41%的剪枝率。

Conclusion: 所提出的全局加权剪枝方法在二值化神经网络中显著优于现有方法，表明跨抽象层级的参数重要性评估是提升二值化神经网络剪枝效率的关键方向。

Abstract: Extreme compression of deep neural networks, up to full binarization, dramatically reduces memory footprint and arithmetic complexity, facilitating deployment on constrained edge hardware with field-programmable gate arrays (FPGAs) and microcontrollers. Although combining binarization with pruning promises additional efficiency gains, existing pruning strategies are ill-suited to binarized representations and rarely translate into meaningful hardware savings. We introduce a PyTorch-based, research-oriented framework that incorporates freezing and pruning mechanisms for designing and optimizing binarized neural networks. The framework enables rapid and reproducible evaluation of state-of-the-art approaches and the fast prototyping of new ones. Leveraging this framework, we propose a novel pruning method that accounts for the relative importance of learned parameters across abstraction levels. Such a global weighting mechanism consistently achieves a superior trade-off between model accuracy and pruning rate, achieving a 70% pruning rate on VGG11 with constant accuracy, while state-of-the-art results reach only 41% in the binarized setting.

</details>


### [330] [Muon with Finite Newton-Schulz: The Smoothing Benefit in Nonsmooth Nonconvex Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26288)
*Mingyi Li,Taira Tsuchiya*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文通过在线到非凸转换框架分析Muon优化器，证明有限次Newton-Schulz迭代（而非精确极分解）对非光滑非凸优化是有益的：它将不连续的极映射平滑为Lipschitz映射，只需对数深度的Newton-Schulz即可保证收敛到稳定点，而精确极分解版本的Muon可能不收敛。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Muon作为大语言模型预训练中矩阵参数优化的强优化器，其Newton-Schulz迭代已被广泛使用。然而现有理论要么用精确极分解替代该迭代，要么将其视为近似误差，导致实际运行的有限深度迭代只会损害理论保证。这与实际观察到的良好表现相矛盾，需要重新审视有限Newton-Schulz的理论价值。

Method: 采用online-to-nonconvex转换框架，将Muon更新规则视为在线学习器，将遗憾界转换为稳定点保证。核心洞见是：有限Newton-Schulz迭代将不连续的极映射平滑为关于奇异值的Lipschitz映射，使Muon可视为具有平滑谱势的在线学习器。作者证明Newton-Schulz深度只需随目标精度对数增长即可保证非光滑非凸优化收敛，并将论证推广到具有相同平滑性质的一般谱映射。

Result: 证明了有限Newton-Schulz迭代Muon在非光滑非凸优化中收敛到稳定点，所需迭代深度仅与目标精度成对数关系；样本复杂度与已知最好的非光滑非凸优化界限匹配，在光滑非凸优化中对到问题相关因子达到最优。相比之下，使用精确极分解的Muon可能无法收敛。

Conclusion: 有限Newton-Schulz迭代的平滑效应是Muon在实践中有效的关键理论来源，而非先前认为的近似误差。该分析框架可扩展至具有平滑性质的一般谱映射族，为理解谱方法类优化器在非凸非光滑场景中的行为提供了新视角。

Abstract: Muon has emerged as a strong optimizer for the matrix-valued parameters in large language model pretraining, approximately orthogonalizing its momentum with a few Newton-Schulz iterations. Existing theory either replaces this iteration with the exact polar factor it approximates, or treats its finite depth as an approximation error, and thus the iteration Muon actually runs can only hurt the guarantees. We show that finite Newton-Schulz can instead be beneficial for nonsmooth nonconvex optimization. To this end, we analyze Muon through the online-to-nonconvex conversion, which views the update rule as an online learner and converts its regret bound into a stationarity guarantee. The finite Newton-Schulz iteration smooths the discontinuous polar map into a Lipschitz map of the singular values, and Muon with finite Newton-Schulz can be regarded as an online learner with a smoothed spectral potential. This smoothing is exactly what the conversion needs: we prove that a Newton-Schulz depth growing only logarithmically in the target accuracy suffices for convergence to stationary points in nonsmooth nonconvex optimization, whereas Muon with the exact-polar update may fail to converge. The resulting sample complexity bounds match the best-known guarantees for nonsmooth nonconvex optimization and are optimal for smooth nonconvex optimization up to problem-dependent factors. The argument extends beyond Newton-Schulz to general spectral maps with the same smoothing property.

</details>


### [331] [Algebraic Multigrid Acceleration for Efficient Label Spreading](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26309)
*Antonia van Betteray,Jonathan Klees,Miriam Schäfers,Matthias Rottmann*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出AMELS框架，通过快速构建邻接图和代数多重网格求解器来加速标签传播算法，实现在大规模高维数据集上的高效半监督学习。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标签传播作为一种半监督学习方法，虽然有效但在大规模高维数据集上受限于计算成本和内存约束，亟需可扩展的解决方案。

Method: AMELS包含两个核心创新：(1) 快速构建邻接图以降低图构建开销；(2) 引入代数多重网格迭代求解器替代传统的随机游走迭代，利用其多层级特性在单个多重网格周期内完成任意规模图上的标签传播。

Result: 与现有实现相比，AMELS实现了显著的运行时间缩减，并且在运行时间和分类准确率方面对超参数选择更具鲁棒性。

Conclusion: AMELS框架使得标签传播能够高效应用于大规模图像数据集，即使在只有少量标注样本的情况下也能产生准确的标签。

Abstract: Modern machine learning models rely on large amounts of labeled data. However, manual annotation of large-scale datasets is expensive and time-consuming. Label spreading is a semi-supervised learning technique that addresses this challenge by propagating information from a few labeled examples to a larger pool of unlabeled data. Despite its effectiveness, its application to large-scale, high-dimensional datasets is limited by computational costs and memory constraints. To address these limitations, we propose Algebraic Multigrid Acceleration for Efficient Label Spreading (AMELS), an efficient label spreading framework that improves scalability by fast construction of neighborhood graphs and the incorporation of algebraic multigrid solvers. The latter is an iterative solver that replaces the ordinary random walk iteration typically performed in label spreading. Due to the multilevel nature of algebraic multigrid solvers, AMELS spreads given label information across a graph of any size in a single multigrid cycle. We demonstrate that AMELS achieves significant runtime reductions compared to existing implementations while also being more robust to hyperparameter choices in terms of both runtime and classification accuracy. Our framework therefore enables efficient label spreading on large-scale image datasets and produces accurate labels even when only a few labeled samples are available.

</details>


### [332] [Privacy Without Regret: Differentially Private Inference-Time Alignment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26324)
*Ishi Jain,Nandini Bhattad,Sayak Ray Chowdhury*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出两种带隐私保护的Best-of-N推断时对齐方法：PrivBoN通过在奖励分数上加Gumbel噪声同时实现ε-差分隐私和KL正则化对齐；PrivITP结合χ²正则化拒绝采样和两阶段高斯机制，实现与响应数量无关的(ε,δ)-DP，并解耦正则化参数与隐私参数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Best-of-N采样是最广泛使用的推断时对齐策略，但存在两个问题：奖励黑客（proxy奖励模型误差被利用）以及敏感人类偏好数据的隐私泄露风险。现有方法无法同时解决这两个问题。

Method: 1) PrivBoN：在奖励分数上添加合适尺度的Gumbel噪声，同时实现ε-差分隐私和KL正则化对齐；2) PrivITP：结合χ²正则化拒绝采样与两阶段高斯机制，实现ex-post (ε,δ)-DP，隐私成本与候选响应数n无关。

Result: 实验表明PrivBoN和PrivITP均具有缩放单调性（而BoN在超过临界n后性能退化）；PrivITP在同等隐私水平下匹配或优于PrivBoN，尤其在强隐私区获益最大。理论结果显示，当隐私预算超过临界阈值ε*时，隐私要求的噪声就是遗憾最优的正则化，隐私不产生额外对齐成本。

Conclusion: 单一的干预手段（向奖励分数添加校准噪声）可以同时解决奖励黑客和隐私泄露两个问题，实现无需额外对齐成本的最优隐私保护对齐。

Abstract: Best-of-N (BoN) sampling is the simplest and most widely deployed inference-time alignment strategy, but it suffers from two distinct problems: reward hacking, in which the selected response exploits errors in the proxy reward model, and the absence of any privacy protection for the sensitive human preference data used to train that reward model. We show that a single intervention-adding calibrated noise to reward scores before selection-resolves both. Our first result, Private Best-of-N (PrivBoN), establishes that Gumbel noise at an appropriate scale simultaneously provides $ε$-differential privacy and implements KL-regularized alignment. Whenever the privacy budget exceeds a critical threshold $ε^*$, the privacy-mandated noise is the regret-optimal regularization, and privacy imposes zero additional alignment cost-matching the information-theoretic skyline of Huang et al. (2025). Because $ε^*$ depends on an unknown coverage coefficient, we introduce Private Inference-Time Pessimism (PrivITP), which combines $χ^2$-regularized rejection sampling with a two-phase Gaussian mechanism. PrivITP achieves ex-post $(ε,δ)$-DP with a privacy cost independent of the number of responses $n$, cleanly decouples the regularization parameter from the privacy parameter, and attains the skyline up to a noise-inflation term. Experiments across several language models, datasets, and reward models confirm our results: PrivBoN and PrivITP are scaling-monotonic (unlike BoN, which degrades past a critical $n$), and PrivITP matches or outperforms PrivBoN at equivalent privacy levels, with the largest gains in the strong-privacy regime.

</details>


### [333] [Beyond Capability Benchmarks: Learning Operational Fingerprints of LLM Cloud Services from Production Incident Metadata](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26332)
*Meiwei Zhang,Eduardo Miranda,Bruce Baynes,Suvigya Jain,Wanlong Chen,Tao He,Sergey Borodavkin*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出OpEmbed框架，利用结构化、隐私保护的工单元数据为LLM云服务学习紧凑的操作指纹（operational fingerprint），无需使用工单文本。在Google Cloud超33,000条覆盖7个LLM家族、26个月的生产工单上验证，OpEmbed能够恢复可解释的家族/版本级结构，提升留一模型操作的预测性能，在数据稀少时依然有效，并支持跨模型故障类型迁移。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现已部署的LLM托管服务在真实生产系统中运行，但模型选型和运维规划仍主要依赖能力基准测试（capability benchmarks），这些基准无法揭示服务部署后的实际运行行为。现有方法缺乏从生产数据中刻画LLM服务操作行为的能力，因此需要一种能基于结构化、隐私保护的元数据学习LLM服务操作特征的方法。

Method: OpEmbed将模型-时间窗口聚合为八通道操作签名（operational signature），并通过时序对比学习（temporal contrastive learning）、跨视图重构（cross-view reconstruction）和代际序正则化（generational-ordinality regularization）学习低维表示。该方法仅使用结构化、隐私保护的工单元数据，避免使用工单文本内容。

Result: 在33,000多条生产工单（覆盖7个LLM家族、26个月）上，OpEmbed成功恢复可解释的家族级和版本级结构；在留一模型（leave-one-model-out）操作预测任务上优于非学习基线；在仅有少量早期窗口数据时依然可用；并支持跨模型的故障类型迁移。

Conclusion: OpEmbed为LLM云服务的运维提供了实用的操作指纹学习框架，可支持模型接入（onboarding）、运维就绪评估（support readiness assessment）和运维监控（operational monitoring），并总结了构建与评估该工具的实际经验教训。

Abstract: Managed LLM services are now part of real production systems, but model selection and service planning still rely heavily on capability benchmarks that reveal little about operational behavior after deployment. We present Operational Embedding (OpEmbed), a framework for learning compact operational fingerprints of LLM cloud services from structured, privacy-preserving support-case metadata, without using case text. OpEmbed aggregates model--time windows into an eight-channel operational signature and learns a low-dimensional representation via temporal contrastive learning, cross-view reconstruction, and generational-ordinality regularization. Evaluated on more than 33,000 production support cases spanning seven LLM families over 26 months at Google Cloud, OpEmbed recovers interpretable family- and version-level structure, improves leave-one-model-out operational forecasting over non-learned baselines, remains useful under limited early-window data, and supports cross-model fault-type transfer. We report the practical lessons learned from building and evaluating this tool for model onboarding, support readiness assessment, and operational monitoring.

</details>


### [334] [CG4AI: A Column Generation Framework for Training AI Models Under Constraints](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26375)
*Youcef Magnouche,Abderrahmane Driouch,Sébastien Martin,Pierre Bauguion*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: CG4AI是一个通过主线性规划与定价子问题构建AI模型凸组合的框架，能够在组合输出上强制执行线性约束，并通过切割平面将可行性保证扩展到训练集之外。在MNIST分类和多商品流问题上验证了其有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准机器学习训练仅最小化数据集上的损失函数，无法保证模型输出满足预定义规则或约束。在自动驾驶、网络路由等真实应用中，这种约束满足的保证至关重要。

Method: CG4AI构建多个AI模型的凸组合，用一个主线性规划优化混合权重，同时约束组合输出满足线性约束；定价子问题利用LP对偶变量生成新模型，重点关注违约最严重的约束；切割平面过程将可行性保证扩展到训练集之外。

Result: 在MNIST上展示了四种约束用途：仅从约束学习、提升对抗鲁棒性、纠正误分类样本、强制输出重标注；在SNDLIB基准网络上验证了多商品流问题的链路容量约束。实验表明CG4AI能可靠生成可行预测器，且准确率优于单模型基线。

Conclusion: CG4AI能够有效地在AI模型组合中强制执行线性约束，在保证可行性的同时提升模型性能，为需要约束满足保证的AI应用提供了一种通用且可靠的解决方案。

Abstract: Standard machine-learning training minimizes a loss function over a dataset, but does not guarantee that the resulting model will satisfy predefined rules or constraints on its outputs. In many real-world applications, ranging from autonomous systems to network routing, such guarantees are essential. We propose CG4AI, a framework that builds a convex combination of AI models while enforcing linear constraints on the combined output. A master linear program (LP) determines the optimal mixture weights, while a pricing subproblem generates new models guided by LP dual variables, focusing attention on the most violated constraints. A cutting-plane procedure extends feasibility guarantees beyond the training set. We apply CG4AI to two problems: (i) digit classification on MNIST, where we demonstrate four distinct uses of constraints, learning from constraints alone, improving adversarial robustness, correcting misclassified examples, and enforcing output relabeling; and (ii) the multi-commodity flow problem, where link capacity constraints are enforced on neural-network routing predictors. Experiments on MNIST and standard SNDLIB benchmark networks show that CG4AI reliably produces feasible predictors while achieving better accuracy than single-model baselines.

</details>


### [335] [The Latent Diagnostic Taxonomy: A Framework for Constructing Classifiers and Diagnosing Their Decisions, Applied to Prompt Injection Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26423)
*Jaturong Kongmanee,Smile Thanapattheerakul*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出"潜在诊断分类法"框架，用于构建分类器作为安全防护层，并开发补充性诊断工具识别哪些高置信决策可被信任。该框架包含维度优化的分类器构建、潜在支持向量定位和诊断分类法构建三步，并在提示注入数据集上验证发现约77%的高置信决策对单token移除不鲁棒。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 分类器作为安全防护层时，其高置信决策未必可靠，模型可能因启发式偏置或脆弱校准而产生错误判断。需要一种系统化的诊断方法来区分"可安全依赖"与"需要人工审查"的决策场景。

Method: 框架包含三步：(1)构建维度优化分类器，通过交叉验证选择嵌入维度而非预设固定值；(2)定位约29%训练样本的潜在支持向量，作为识别可改变预测标签的关键token；(3)利用这些token及其攻击强度构建诊断分类法，将提示分为可安全依赖、启发式偏置/覆盖、上下文不足需人工审查等类别。

Result: 在公开的提示注入数据集上，发现约77%的高置信决策在移除单个token后不再鲁棒，该脆弱性可分为两种失败模式：置信度校准失败和可利用捷径。分类法各区域均有对应的修复策略建议。

Conclusion: 该框架提供端到端的诊断指南，可为分类器高置信决策划分不同处理路径，并为每种诊断结果推荐针对性的修复策略，从而提升安全防护层的可靠性和可解释性。

Abstract: This paper proposes a framework for constructing a classifier as a safeguard layer, and for developing a complementary diagnostic that identifies which of the classifier's confident decisions can be trusted. This framework, the Latent Diagnostic Taxonomy, consists of (i) constructing a dimensionality-optimized classifier, in which the embedding dimensionality is empirically selected via cross-validated performance rather than fixed a priori, (ii) locating a relatively small set of latent support vectors (~ 29% of total training examples) representing influential prompts for identifying tokens that alter the classifier's predicted labels, and (iii) utilizing such tokens and their associated attack magnitudes for constructing a diagnostic taxonomy. This diagnostic taxonomy provides an end-to-end guideline for flagging prompts that require different treatments: rely Safely on the classifier's decision; flag Heuristic Bias and Heuristic Override cases; route Insufficient Context cases for further human/safety review. Applying the framework to a classifier trained on a public prompt injection dataset, we find that a substantial fraction of its confident decisions (~ 77%) are not robust to removing a single token, and that this brittleness separates into two distinct failure patterns: a confidence calibration failure and a genuinely exploitable shortcut. For each zone of the taxonomy, we also recommend strategies for remediating diagnosed prompts. We illustrate the framework as a series of steps, demonstrating how each step operates.

</details>


### [336] [FedCMAPSS: A Benchmark for Federated Learning in Remaining Useful Life Estimation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26433)
*Amelia Sorrenti,Matteo Pennisi,Concetto Spampinato,Simone Palazzo*
